# TREVOR Hub Dashboard — System State

> **Last rebuilt: 2026-05-16.** CC's current-state reference for the Hub — read top-to-bottom in Phase 0 of every Hub prompt. Hard ceiling: under 300 lines.
> Hub-specific only. Bot-side engineering rules, the feature registry, and recurring bugs live in `/home/trevor/trevor/BEHAVIOR_RULES.md` + `/home/trevor/trevor/CLAUDE.md` — read those; this file does not restate them.
> Prior session history is archived to `docs/SESSION_HISTORY.md` (historical, not active documentation).

---

## Two-Box Topology

> Current-state operating layout (post-migration). TREVOR runs on **two boxes / two Termius tabs**. Some older dated entries below predate the migration and describe the Hub co-residing with the bot on the VM — those are historical records, not the current layout.

- **This box = TrevorHub (the "WSL Hub").** Ghost's PC — `ghost@Ghost`, hostname `Ghost`, tailnet `100.125.247.52`. **One machine, two names:** also reachable as **`trevorhub-wsl`** via Tailscale MagicDNS (same box). Runs the **Hub dashboard** (Next.js 15) at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`. This is Ghost's default cockpit — monitoring, audits, recon, dashboard work.
- **The other box = the VM.** `trevor@trevor-prime`, **GCP e2-standard-2** (8 GB RAM, 2 vCPU), tailnet `100.93.113.117`, domain **`trevor-prime.com`**. Runs the **live bot** (`trevor.service`), the exit engine, signals, and the live **`trevor.db`**. All bot code lives here — bot repo `/home/trevor/trevor`, branch `main`.
- **The pipe (`ssh vm`).** From this WSL tab, **`ssh vm`** reaches the VM (login user `ghost`, passwordless sudo, trevor-group). Use it **read-only** — "run a command on the VM, read the result": audits, status, recon. **NOT** for editing VM files over the pipe; bot-file edits run in the **VM tab** directly.
- **The live `trevor.db` lives on the VM — never on this PC.** Network SQLite = corruption + latency risk. Dashboard reads reach it through the **gateway/pipe**, not a local DB copy.

---

## Tech Stack

- **Next.js 15.3.3** (App Router) · **React 19.0.0** · **TypeScript 5.9.3** · **Tailwind CSS 4** (CSS-first; design tokens live in `globals.css @theme`).
- Repo: `/home/trevor/trevor-dashboard/`, git branch **`master`**.
- Served by a custom **`server.js`** — `npm start` runs `node server.js` (*not* `next start`) on port **3333**, bound to `127.0.0.1` only. Dev server: `npm run dev` (`next dev -H 127.0.0.1 -p 3333`).
- **nginx** reverse proxy (ports 80/443, Let's Encrypt SSL) → **https://trevor-prime.com**. `middleware.ts` 301-redirects direct-IP hits to the domain.
- systemd unit: **`trevor-dashboard.service`**.
- Auth: a single cookie `trevor_session` — **HMAC-SHA256-signed, expiring token** (`base64url(JSON{u,iat,exp}) . base64url(HMAC)`, key = **env-only `SESSION_SALT`**, 7-day `exp`), verified server-side (signature + expiry, constant-time compare) in `middleware.ts` + `/api/auth` via the shared `src/lib/session-token.ts` — no network call. **Fail-closed** if `SESSION_SALT` is unset/short (no default). (W-D-P1, 2026-06-07 — replaced the legacy reversible `user:pass:salt` token + the hardcoded `trevor-mc-2025` fallback.)
- `layout.tsx` wraps every page in `<ThemeProvider>` + `<AppShell>`. Chat is direct-to-Anthropic via `chat_bridge.py` / `chat_ai.py` — not Discord, not any external workflow tool.
- The Hub has **no database of its own** — it reads the bot's SQLite at `/home/trevor/trevor/trevor.db`. Local file state lives in `data/` (`chat-sessions/`, `watchlist-meta.json`).
- Shared code: `src/lib/` (`api-helpers.ts` Python bridge, `feature-flags.ts`, `fetch.ts`, `format.ts`, `navigation.ts`, `a11y.ts`, `anthropic-key.ts`, `utils.ts`) and `src/hooks/` (`useLongPress`, `usePullToRefresh`, `useScrollDirection`).
- The Hub never calls the bot over HTTP — every bot fact comes from reading `trevor.db` and brain files on the shared filesystem.
- Request flow: `middleware.ts` (auth gate) → page or `api/*/route.ts` → `runPython()` → a root-level `*.py` script → `trevor.db`.

## Route Structure

App Router pages under `src/app/`, verified against the filesystem:

| Path | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `/autotrader` | AutoTrader — 5-tab board: **Dashboard** (default) · **Recent** · **Config** · **Control** · **Activity**. **Hub landing** — `/` redirects here (Wave B1). Restructured from single-view to 5-tab shell in Wave C1 (2026-05-27); Config/Control/Activity are placeholders until D1/D2/D3 ship. |
| `/stocks` | Stock signals + DCA reminders — Stock / DCA sub-tabs (was `/manual`, Wave C2) |
| `/stocks/scout` | SCOUT discovery feed |
| `/intel` | Notes / Shadow / Lessons / Journal (Lessons + Journal moved back from `/docs` 2026-05-19; Similar + Calibration removed and Notes added as default 2026-05-20 — Notes is a client-side notepad, localStorage `trevor-hub-notes`, no API) |
| `/docs` | Downloads file browser with category tabs — single-view zone since Lessons / Journal moved back to `/intel` 2026-05-19; gated `HUB_REDESIGN_DOCS` (ON) |
| `/memory` | Brain / Memory / ChromaDB / System Health / Aggressive |
| `/chat` | TREVOR chat (direct Anthropic API) |
| `/login` | Cookie auth |
| `/design-system` | A4 primitive showcase (internal) |

- App-level files in `src/app/`: `layout.tsx` (shell), `error.tsx` (error boundary), `not-found.tsx` (404), `globals.css`.
- Every page except `/login` is auth-gated by `middleware.ts`; an unauthenticated page request 302-redirects to `/login?from=<path>`, and unauthenticated `/api/*` calls get a 401.
- Redirect-only pages: `/` → `/autotrader`; `/brain` → `/control` (chains through to `/memory`).
- Legacy redirects in `middleware.ts` (308): `/trading`, `/scalp` & `/manual` → `/stocks` (Wave C2 — direct single hop, no chaining), `/command` → `/memory`, `/intelligence` → `/intel`, `/dashboard` → `/autotrader` (HOME page retired, Wave B1).
- Legacy redirects in `next.config.ts` (308): `/trades` `/holdings` `/signals` `/research` `/training` `/control` `/ghost` `/dev-tasks` `/reminders` → a retired zone path + `?tab=` (then re-chained by `middleware.ts`); plus 3 `/api/auto-trader/*` → `/api/auto/*`.
- Sub-tabs are `?tab=` query params only — no nested routes except `/stocks/scout`. Per zone: autotrader → `dashboard`/`recent`/`config`/`control`/`activity` (`dashboard` default, Wave C1); stocks → `stock`/`dca`; intel → `notes`/`shadow`/`lessons`/`journal` (`notes` default); memory → `brain`/`memory`/`chroma`/`health`/`aggressive`. (docs is single-view — no zone-level sub-tabs; docs has its own internal category tab strip inside the Downloads section from Wave B1.)

## API Routes

68 `route.ts` files under `src/app/api/`, grouped by zone (Wave E2 deleted 7 manual-scalp routes; Wave RM-AH-E2 deleted 3 duplicate/dead routes; B3 added `auto/exit-controls` 2026-05-27):

- **Dashboard** — `status`, `live`, `dashboard/calibration`, `stats/daily-pnl`, `heartbeat`, `prices`, `nav-badges`. (Wave B1 dropped the `/dashboard` page and its `active`/`pnl`/`edge`/`quick-stats` API routes. `dashboard/calibration` is kept but now orphaned — its sole consumer, the SCALP calibration section, was deleted in Wave C2; retained pending a cleanup wave.)
- **AutoTrader** — `auto/{state,config,trades,exit-controls,profit-risk,leverage-regime}`, `capital`, `circuit-breaker`, `entry-preflight`, `exit-signals`, `analytics/{confidence-tiers,duration,regime-performance}`, `time-slots`, `trade-stats`. (`auto/profit-risk` — S1-P06, READ-ONLY: per open-trade exit posture [breakeven armed?, ratchet floor R, partials taken + realized partial P&L, intended risk $/%] + consolidated circuit-breaker state [entries_allowed?, active breakers, each breaker's reading vs limit]. Backed by `query_profit_risk.py` which reads `auto_trades` (sqlite RO) + `circuit_breaker.CircuitBreakerSystem().get_status()`; 10s in-route cache, fail-safe shape. Never mutates / sends bot commands.)
- **Manual / Trades** — `signals/unread-count`, `trades` + `trades/{close,close-status,command-status,edit-entry,flip}`, `watchlist`, `reminders`, `dca`. (Wave E2 deleted the `signals` parent route, `live-board` + `live-board/{enter,ticker}`, and `trades/{add-position,partial-close,tranches}` with the manual-scalp helper teardown — `signals/unread-count` kept, it backs the nav unread badges.)
- **Intel** — `intel/{lessons,journal,shadow,downloads}`, `journal`, `quality`, `training`. (Wave 2026-05-20 deleted `intel/similar/[source]/[id]` and `intel/calibration`; Wave 2026-05-26 deleted `intel/optuna` with the Optuna A/B removal; Notes is client-side and has no API.)
- **Docs** — `docs/categories` (GET list + POST create), `docs/categories/[id]` (PUT rename / DELETE), `docs/categories/reorder` (PUT), `docs/downloads/[filename]/move` (PUT). Downloads category/folder system — all route through `query_docs_categories.py` → bot-side `download_manager` (backend, 2026-05-19; frontend lands in B1).
- **Memory** — `memory/{brain,chroma,daily,health,journal,aggressive,autotrader-toggle}`, `brain`, `system-health`, `aggressive`.
- **Chat** — `chat`, `chat/{stream,budget,suggestions}`.
- **Control / Admin** — `killswitch`, `admin/{current-state,reset-capital,reset-pnl-stats}`, `feature-flags`, `commits`, `auth`, `health`.

Conventions:
- Handlers call `runPython()`, parse its stdout as JSON, and return that. Live-data routes set `export const dynamic = "force-dynamic"` to bypass caching; several wrap the call in try/catch and return a fail-safe JSON shape instead of throwing.
- About 30 of the 70 accept `POST` (writes/actions); the rest are GET-only reads.
- `chat/stream` is the one streaming endpoint — it returns a `ReadableStream` (SSE); every other route returns plain JSON.
- `admin/*` routes are destructive resets (capital / P&L stats / history) — POST-gated, Ghost-only. `commits` reads recent git history for the in-app deploy widget.
- High-traffic reads: `live` (full dashboard payload), `status` (service state + signal counts), `nav-badges` (bottom-nav unread counts), `heartbeat` (Observatory pulse).
- Dynamic segments: `intel/journal/[source]/[id]`, `memory/brain/[name]`, `memory/daily/[date]`, `quality/[id]`, `intel/downloads/[filename]`, `docs/categories/[id]`, `docs/downloads/[filename]/move`.

## Python Helper Scripts

The Hub has no direct SQLite binding — every DB read/write goes through Python scripts at the repo root. Verified counts:

- **`query_*.py` × 41** (was 43; QUAL-05 2026-06-03 deleted the orphan `query_similar_trades.py` + `query_calibration_deep.py`, also untracked from the bot's `TRACKED_HELPERS`) — MOSTLY read-only (`trevor.db` opened `mode=ro`); Wave E2 deleted 6 manual-scalp query helpers; S2-P05 added `query_leverage_regime.py`. Notable: `query_brain.py`, `query_training.py`, `query_feature_flags.py` (the last backs `/api/feature-flags`). **⚠️ NOT all `query_*.py` are read-only** (QUAL-04, 2026-06-03): **9 carry WRITE paths** (open a *separate* rw `sqlite3.connect(db_path)` without `mode=ro`, or delegate to a bot writer) for legitimate action endpoints — `query_trades.py` (DELETE `active_trades`/`trade_outcomes`/`training_learned_outcomes` + UPDATE `training_status` — the trade-delete + training-status feature), `query_close.py` (INSERT `close_requests`), `query_edit_entry.py` (UPDATE `active_trades`), `query_hub_commands.py` (INSERT `hub_commands`), `query_admin_reset.py` (INSERT `capital_resets` + REPLACE `trevor_config`), `query_capital.py` (REPLACE `trevor_config`), `query_quality.py` (UPDATE `quality_patterns`), `query_journal_narrative.py` (INSERT/DELETE `trade_journal` + UPDATE `auto_config`), `query_chat_budget.py` (UPDATE/INSERT `auto_config`). Don't assume a `query_*.py` is read-only — check.
- **`set_*.py` × 5** — WRITE: `set_killswitch.py`, `set_aggressive.py`, `set_autotrader_enabled.py`, `set_dca.py`, `set_reminders.py`.
- **`write_*.py` × 2** — WRITE: `write_brain_file.py`, `write_chat_log.py`.
- **`manage_*.py` / `chat_*.py`** — `manage_{brain,portfolio,schedule,watchlist}.py`, `chat_bridge.py`, `chat_ai.py`.

Invoked from `src/lib/api-helpers.ts`:
- **`runPython(script, args)`** — `spawnSync` with an argv array: **no shell, no string interpolation**, so user input is injection-safe (it never reaches a shell). Synchronous — **never `await` it**. Default timeout 15 s, `maxBuffer` 10 MB; runs with `cwd=/home/trevor/trevor` and `HOME=/home/trevor`.
- **`runPythonInline(code)`** — runs a snippet via `python3 -` (stdin); untrusted values must be passed through the env map, never interpolated into the code string.
- A non-zero exit becomes a thrown `Error` carrying the first 500 chars of stderr; scripts must print one JSON object to stdout. Python binary: `/home/trevor/trevor/venv/bin/python3`.

## Design System

- A4 tokens live in `globals.css` under `@theme inline` — a parallel namespace. **Locked** to extension by surgical A4-revision prompts only (most recent: A4 v1.1 Hub Design Foundation, 2026-05-21).
- A4 v1 token families: `--color-*`, `--shadow-glow-*`, `--radius-*` (incl. `--radius-pill`), `--transition-duration-*` (instant 80 ms / fast 160 ms / medium 240 ms / slow 400 ms), `--breakpoint-*` (`xs` 375 px, `mm` 430 px), `--animate-*`.
- **A4 v1.1 refined family (additive, 2026-05-21).** Parallel to v1 — both render concurrently. Refined accents `--color-accent-{cyan-soft, plum, mint, gold}` with `*-strong` / `*-subtle` siblings (red kept; only `-strong`/`-subtle` added). Subtle glow utilities `--shadow-glow-{subtle, active, focus}` + per-color subtle/active glows (~30-40% of v1 neon intensity). Geist Sans loaded via `next/font/google` in `layout.tsx` — `--font-sans` token → Tailwind `font-sans` utility. Opt-in type utilities `.text-{label,heading,h1,h2,prose,caption}-ui`. Tab primitive (`tab-bar-v2.tsx`) carries a 28px mask-image edge-fade on overflow + refined active-tab visual. New card utility classes `.card-{base,elevated,active,warn}`. `<Pill>` primitive gains `intent` prop (`blue-chip` / `mid-cap` / `meme` / `live` / `active` / `running` / `warn` / `error`). **One-page reference: `src/styles/design-tokens.md`.** Revamp complete end-to-end as of 2026-05-21 (A1 foundation → B1 Auto+Stocks+Docs → B2 Intel+Memory+Chat+Header → C1 login+password+nav+cross-page+mobile). Body font default stays mono (sans is opt-in per surface).
- **Standing aesthetic preference (confirmed 2026-05-21 C1).** Hub aesthetic is **refined dark cyber** — desaturated accents (~50% saturation), subtle glows (~30-40% of original neon intensity), mono for data (prices, P&L, timestamps, tickers, hashes), sans-serif for UI labels (nav, tabs, buttons, headings, prose, form labels), professional polish over arcade neon. Refined cyan-soft is the default UI accent; refined mint = financial green / LIVE / ACTIVE / RUNNING; refined plum substitutes magenta for restrained mid-cap / meme tiers; refined gold replaces electric amber for non-critical warnings; red stays unchanged (critical attention only). Future Hub work follows this aesthetic by default — surfaces should opt into refined tokens, not legacy v1 electric tokens.
- Legacy tokens stay under `:root` (`--background`, `--card`, `--neon-*`, `--glow-*`, `--sidebar`) and still drive pre-A4 components; all namespaces coexist deliberately.
- Cyberpunk palette (A4 v1, still live): primary/brand **cyan `#00f0ff`**; neon accents — green `#00ff88`, magenta `#ff00ff`, amber `#ffaa00`, red `#ff3366`, violet `#b478ff`. Backgrounds `#0a0a0f` / `#12121a`. The `html.oled` class swaps to true-black.
- Fonts: **JetBrains Mono** (`@font-face` from gstatic, weights 400/700, `--font-mono`) — body default; **Geist Sans** (`next/font/google`, variable, `--font-sans`) — opt-in for UI labels/headings/prose.
- **19 `@keyframes`** in `globals.css` — legacy (`shake`, `pulseLive`, `scanline`, `ticker-scroll`, `quickJumpIn`, …) and A4 (`pulse-cyan/amber/green/magenta`, `shimmer-ds`, `slide-up`, `fade-in`, `slide-up-spring`, `slide-down-spring`, `scrim-fade-in`).
- **20 primitives** in `src/components/ui/`, including trading-domain ones — `direction-badge`, `money-text`, `confidence-bar`, `killswitch-pill`, `live-pulse`. `tw-animate-css` is imported; dark styling uses the `@custom-variant dark` selector.
- `/design-system` renders every `ui/` primitive live — review it before building a new one. Mobile-interaction hooks: `useLongPress`, `usePullToRefresh`, `useScrollDirection`.
- The "live data" affordance is `live-pulse` + the `pulse-*` keyframes — reuse it rather than rolling a new indicator.
- Mobile-first — verify every layout at **375 px** (the `xs` breakpoint) before calling it done.
- Bottom nav: 5 zones — Auto, Stocks, Intel, Docs, Memory (Wave B3 dropped the HOME slot; Wave C2 renamed Manual→Stocks; Wave D3 added the Docs slot). Chat is a floating action button, not a tab.

## Feature Flags

`HUB_REDESIGN_*` flags are rows in the `auto_config` DB table. The Hub reads them via `/api/feature-flags` → `query_feature_flags.py` (fail-safe: any error → all-off → old layout). A flag flip is a direct `auto_config` write — picked up within seconds on the next read, no restart needed. The `hub_redesign_override=` cookie lets Ghost preview a flag per-session without flipping the DB (format: `hub_redesign_override=HUB_REDESIGN_CHAT=true`, comma-separate multiple).

Live values, verified from `auto_config` on 2026-05-16:

| Flag | Value | Gates |
|---|---|---|
| `HUB_REDESIGN_MODE` | `false` | master switch for the whole redesign |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_NAV` | `true` | redesigned nav shell (sidebar + bottom bar) |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_DASHBOARD` | `true` | rebuilt `/dashboard` |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_AUTO` | `true` | AutoTrader v2 (`autotrader-v2/`) |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_AUTO_API` | `true` | consolidated `/api/auto/*` endpoints |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_SCALP` | `true` | gates the `/stocks` page — legacy flag name kept (Wave C2 renamed the zone, not the inert `auto_config` key; additive-DB rule) |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_INTEL` | `true` | rebuilt `/intel` sections |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_MEMORY` | `true` | rebuilt `/memory` sections |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_CHAT` | `false` | new chat surface |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_DOCS` | `true` | gates the `/docs` page — Downloads / Lessons / Journal live (Wave D2) |
| `SCOUT_V3_FEED` | `true` | SCOUT discovery feed v3 |

Eight wave flags are live; `MODE` (master) and `CHAT` remain off. Note: `feature-flags.ts:readFlag()` is a leftover stub that always returns `false` — `/api/feature-flags` is the real source.

## Deploy Checklist

Commit first — explicit `git add <files>` (never `-A`) on branch `master` — then:

1. `npm run build` (`next build`) — must succeed.
2. `npx tsc --noEmit` — must pass.
3. `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` — **required after every build.** The running Next.js server caches its build manifest in memory; rebuilding without a restart leaves it serving HTML that references deleted fingerprinted CSS/JS → 400/404 on every asset, pages render unstyled. After the restart, check `journalctl -u trevor-dashboard.service -n 30` for boot errors before moving on.
4. `bash verify_deploy.sh` — ~50 smoke checks: service health, page routes, API GETs, the brain-write 423 gate, the unauth 401 gate, chat-budget gate, recurring-bug canaries, litestream. It exits non-zero if any check fails, and still probes the retired `ghost-qa.service`, so expect one pre-existing FAIL — judge the run on the rest.
5. Confirm the change live at https://trevor-prime.com.

Ad-hoc route test (auth is a cookie):
```bash
curl -s -c /tmp/c -X POST localhost:3333/api/auth -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d "{\"action\":\"login\",\"username\":\"trevor\",\"password\":\"$(grep ^DASHBOARD_PASS= .env.local|cut -d= -f2-)\"}"
curl -s -b /tmp/c localhost:3333/api/status
```

No service restarts mid-task — restart only at step 3, and only with Ghost approval.

## Known Issues

- `feature-flags.ts:readFlag()` is a stub that always returns `false`; the real flag source is `/api/feature-flags`. Components must not call `readFlag()` directly.
- `src/app/api/system-health/route.ts` killswitch readout reads `auto_config.EMERGENCY_KILLSWITCH` (canonical state). The historical dead `.kill_switch` flat-file read was removed by RM-AH-P03 (commit `28daed0`) and again hardened by QUAL-01 (2026-06-03) to **fail SAFE** — on a DB read error it reports `{"active": null, "status": "unknown"}`, never a false `active: false` all-clear. (Prior note here claimed `route.ts:81` still read the flat file — that was stale; the flat file no longer exists and nothing reads it.) Note: no Hub component currently consumes this route's `kill_switch` field — the `/memory?tab=health` killswitch tile is sourced from `/api/killswitch` (`<KillswitchControlCard>`) + `/api/heartbeat` (`<HeartbeatView>`).
- `next.config.ts` legacy redirects target retired zone names (`/trading`, `/intelligence`, `/command`) and only resolve because `middleware.ts` re-chains them — editing either file alone can silently break legacy links.
- `verify_deploy.sh` still probes `ghost-qa.service` (retired) and the pre-rename `/scalp?tab=*` paths — the service check is a pre-existing FAIL; the page checks pass only because the 308 redirect is accepted.
- `verify_deploy.sh` briefly force-mutates two `auto_config` rows (`HUB_BRAIN_EDIT_ENABLED`, `ANTHROPIC_API_DAILY_TOKENS_USED`) during its gate tests and restores them — don't run it alongside a real config edit.
- `middleware.ts` hardcodes the VM IP `34.28.231.36` for the direct-IP→domain redirect; a VM IP change silently breaks it.
- `/chat` injects a page-scoped `<style>` block overriding the global theme (Termius-blue) — a deliberate zone-local exception to the design tokens.
- `change-password-modal.tsx` is present but historically buggy (field-name mismatch) — verify before relying on it.
- `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` and `.env.local` are perpetually modified in `git status`; the repo root also carries stale `*.bak` files — never stage any of them in a selective commit.

## Hub-Specific Rules

- **`trevor.db` belongs to the bot; the Hub reads it `mode=ro` but DOES write a defined set of tables** through dedicated action helpers (the `set_*.py`/`write_*.py`/`manage_*.py` writers + the 9 writing `query_*.py` above + one inline TS writer in `trades/route.ts`). The reconciled real **write surface = ~18 `trevor.db` tables** (QUAL-04, 2026-06-03 — supersedes the old "writes happen only through ~30 POST routes / treat everything else read-only" framing, which understated it): **`auto_config`** (flags/killswitch via the bot module/aggressive/toggles/budgets) · **`autotrader_state_audit`** · **`change_log`** (audit envelope via `audit_logger`) · **`aggressive_mode_history`** · **`hub_commands`** · **`active_trades`** (margin edit / entry edit / delete-closed) · **`close_requests`** · **`trade_outcomes`** · **`training_learned_outcomes`** · **`trade_journal`** · **`capital_resets`** · **`trevor_config`** · **`quality_patterns`** · **`portfolio_positions`** · **`watchlist`** · **`brain_edit_audit`** · **`chat_sessions`** · **`chat_messages`** — plus `dca_schedule` (via `DCAManager`) + reminders (via `set_reminders`). All are legitimate user/action features; **no illegitimate write exists** (the DELETEs are row-level delete-feature ops, NOT destructive *schema* edits, so the additive-schema rule below still holds). Every OTHER route stays read-only; new writes need a dedicated helper + this list updated.
- **WAL ownership:** litestream/writer owns checkpointing; the Hub **never** runs `wal_checkpoint` and opens all *reads* `mode=ro` (its writes use short-lived rw connections through the helpers above — "read-only on `trevor.db`" here means **checkpoint-ownership + reads**, not "no writes"). WAL bloat / TRUNCATE-blocked cadence is a writer/litestream concern (bot repo, RM-SYS) — not a Hub fix.
- **Read-replica is SELF-REFRESHING, never frozen (W-H-P2-HUB, 2026-06-09).** `/home/ghost/trevor-replica/trevor.db` is **continuously restored from GCS**: `trevor-restore.timer` → `trevor-restore.service` (oneshot, `deploy/scripts/trevor-restore.sh`) runs `litestream restore` from `gcs://trevor-prime-backups/litestream/trevor.db` every ~15 min (`OnUnitInactiveSec`; a full restore ≈8–14 min, so effective freshness ~20–30 min), publishing a self-contained **DELETE-mode** file via atomic `mv` — the Hub's per-request `mode=ro` opens pick it up with **no restart**. **One-way, pull-only** (GCS→WSL); it NEVER pushes. The old `litestream.service` is a *replicate*/PUSH unit — now **disabled + masked**, and `litestream.yml` renamed `…DISABLED-replicate-DANGER`, so nothing can fork the VM's live GCS generation. The script's `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` runs **only on the throwaway staging copy** (to publish one clean file) — it does NOT touch the live read path or the VM/GCS chain, so the "Hub never checkpoints" rule above still holds. Efficiency win (shrink each restore to seconds) = shorten the **VM-side** litestream `snapshot-interval` — a separate, VM-side follow-up, NOT done here. Runbook: `docs/hub_replica_continuous_restore_2026_06_09.md`.
- **Equity "STALE" badge is an AGE check, not the SWR flag (W-H-P2-HUB, 2026-06-09).** `/api/auto/state` `equity_stale` = `Date.now() - equity_ts > 60s` (was the per-call SWR `stale` flag, which was permanently `true` because the equity cache TTL 10s < client poll 15s). Clears under the normal 15s poll (value stays <60s old); still flags when the heartbeat is genuinely stale >60s, showing the last-known value. `src/app/api/auto/state/route.ts`.
- **The W-H-P2 badge fix was committed but NEVER DEPLOYED — re-fixed by W-H-P3-HUB (2026-06-09).** Why the badge "survived Fix 2": the running Hub served a `.next` build from **13:14:45**, but the age-fix commit `ad6eaad` landed **13:23:07** (8 min later) and nobody ran `npm run build` + restarted. The deployed artifact still had the always-true per-call flag (the source was already correct). **Durable lesson: a Hub *source* fix is inert until `npm run build` AND `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` — verify against the LIVE endpoint at 15s cadence, never just `tsc`/a synthetic check (the P2 synthetic check passed while the bug shipped).** P3 rebuilt+restarted; the badge is now proven `equity_stale:false` at cadence on the live Hub (`ts` advancing), and the genuine-stale path (>60s un-refreshed → `true`) was observed firing live.
- **Floating PnL now mirrors real HL via the heartbeat (W-H-P3-HUB).** The Hub has no HL creds, so `query_auto_state.py:_fetch_hl()` always returns 0 unrealized → the "floating" sub-line on `capital-hero.tsx` showed **$0 even with an open position**. `route.ts:resolveRealHlEquity()` now also reads `categories.autotrader.unrealized_pnl_usd` from the SAME heartbeat (best-effort; absent/flat ⇒ null) and the response computes `unrealized_usd = equity.unrealized ?? value.unrealized_usd`. Same pattern/feed as `account_value_usd`. **Equity-value cadence caveat:** the account value itself is the latest `equity_snapshots.equity` (VM monitor-loop, **~hourly** — see observatory `collect_account_value()`), so the displayed value mirrors real HL only to ~1h granularity; the badge's "fresh" means "the Hub is reading the heartbeat fresh", NOT "tick-by-tick HL". Real-time equity = a separate VM-side follow-up. **Every Hub number was audited against live HL `user_state` + VM `trevor.db` on 2026-06-09 (W-H-P3-HUB) — all match source-of-truth within the published replica (≤15min) and equity-snapshot (~1h) cadences.**
- **Live/open Hub numbers source from the heartbeat; closed history from the replica (W-H-P4-HUB, 2026-06-09).** Cleared the remaining read-side defects from the full HL-mirror audit (display-accuracy only; money path untouched):
  - **Margin utilization (`/api/auto/leverage-regime`) is derived from the heartbeat, not a live HL call.** The Hub has no `hyperliquid` module, so `query_leverage_regime.py:fetch_margin()`'s `info.user_state` read ALWAYS threw `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'hyperliquid'` → the panel was pinned to "margin read degraded". `fetch_margin()` is now a no-op sentinel (no HL import); `route.ts:resolveHeartbeatMargin()` rebuilds the block from `account_value_usd` + per-open-position `notional_usd`/`leverage`: `total_ntl_pos = Σ notional`, `margin_used = Σ(notional/leverage)` (isolated initial margin — the bot's mode), `utilization_pct = margin_used/account_value`. Flat ⇒ 0% / $acct. Live-proven `available:true`, 0% flat. **Do NOT install `hyperliquid` on WSL — the heartbeat is the source.**
  - **`/api/prices` repointed off the dead `:3335` WS.** W-E-P2b moved the Observatory to `:8443`, which serves `/api/heartbeat` but NOT prices/allMids (verified 404), so the `tryWsPrices`/`:3335` path was dead — REMOVED. The 30s HL-REST→CG SWR cache is now the sole/primary source (tracks live HL within ~0.01–0.04%). Per-ticker `stale` is now **age-based** (`Date.now()-ts > PRICE_STALE_AGE_MS = 90s`), not the SWR per-call flag — kills the false-stale blip that fired on the one poll per 30s window landing on the TTL boundary. Live-proven 0/20 polls stale across a boundary crossing. (The audit's "stale:true ALWAYS" was an isolated-sampling artifact; under the real 2s cadence it was already mostly false — the real defects were the dead path + the boundary blip.)
  - **Open-count / open-positions-count / deployed (`open_exposure_usd`) now come from the heartbeat** in `/api/auto/state` (live `categories.autotrader.open_count` + `Σ open_positions[].notional_usd`), NOT the ≤15min litestream replica `query_auto_state.py` reads — kills the transient phantom open (#100444 / $4.33 deployed) that lingered for one restore window after a close. `resolveRealHlEquity()` was extended to carry the open numbers from the SAME heartbeat fetch (one call). **Closed history stays on the replica** (`realized`/`realized_count`/`realized_pct`/`realized_unknown_count`/`trades_total` — fine at 15min). Live-proven 0/0/$0, matching the heartbeat.
  - **`trades_today` = closed-LIVE trades, ET-calendar** (the dashboard canonical = `query_auto_state.py` `realized_count.today`) — this was ALREADY ET-correct (no boundary bug on the dashboard). The memory/health page (`heartbeat-view.tsx`) showed the heartbeat's broader `trades_today` (ALL modes, **UTC day**, incl null-pnl) under the same "Trades today" label → looked like a contradiction (Hub 11/12 vs heartbeat 22/23). Fixed by **relabeling** the heartbeat count to "Closed today · all modes (UTC)" so it reads as a distinct diagnostic, not the same metric. The collector (VM) is untouched.
  - **DURABLE PREFERENCE: Hub LIVE/OPEN numbers (equity, floating, margin, open-count, deployed) source from the Observatory heartbeat; CLOSED history + realized totals stay on the litestream replica.** The heartbeat is live (the VM collector reads the real `trevor.db` each collect); the replica lags ≤15min. Never read an open/live number off the replica (phantom risk); never expect tick-by-tick freshness from the replica.
  - Files: `src/app/api/auto/leverage-regime/route.ts`, `query_leverage_regime.py`, `src/app/api/prices/route.ts`, `src/app/api/auto/state/route.ts`, `src/components/memory/heartbeat-view.tsx`. Rebuilt + restarted `trevor-dashboard.service` (BUILD_ID `NY4mg6y0yKd1dN0HObgPu`). Full HL-mirror audit **closed 2026-06-09**.
- DB changes are **additive** — new rows / new `auto_config` keys, never destructive schema edits. (The only new table in flight is **`gateway_idempotency`** — created `IF NOT EXISTS` on the VM by the W-C-P2a VM gateway.)
- **Dangerous writes go through the two-hop write-gateway (W-C-P2a), not the replica.** The 18 financial/destructive/state routes call `src/lib/gateway-client.ts` → local gateway (`:3939`) → Tailscale → VM gateway → bot helper → live `trevor.db`. The client sends an **op name** (allowlist in `gateway/ops.js`) + validated args, never SQL or a helper path. Flag check + mandatory `change_log` audit + idempotency are enforced **VM-side**. Fail modes: 423 locked · 502 VM unreachable · 504 timeout — **no route returns a raw readonly-DB 500 anymore**. Until the VM gateway is deployed, these writes fail CLOSED at 502/504. Transport is **Tailscale-only** (no nginx/public/non-tailnet fallback). New write surfaces must add an op (both gateways) + keep route-level validation.
- **Gateway health probes (2026-06-07):** unauthenticated **`GET /healthz`** → `200 {"status":"ok"}` (liveness only, no token, reveals nothing else); authed **`GET /gateway/health`** still reports `write_route`/`ops`/`writes_enabled` (bearer required).
- **Benign writes (W-C-P2b, 2026-06-07) — Hub half wired, VM op-map PENDING.** The last 4 benign routes now route writes through the gateway instead of the read-only replica: `/api/trades` PUT→`trades.annotate` (trade_outcomes notes), `/api/reminders`→`reminders.set`, `/api/chat/stream`→`chat.log_user`/`chat.log_assistant`, `/api/intel/journal`→`journal.generate`. Journal **GET is cache-read-only** (generation is POST-only); `query_chat_budget.py` is now **pure-read** (the daily-rollover reset is VM-owned + reaches the replica via litestream — the Hub must never write it). All 5 ops ride **`HUB_BENIGN_WRITE_ENABLED`** (default OFF). **VM follow-up REQUIRED:** add the 5 ops + op→helper map to `vm_gateway.js`, create the flag row, widen the VM forward timeout for `journal.generate` (Haiku call > the 8s `VM_FORWARD_TIMEOUT_MS`). Until it lands these routes return 400/502 (explicit-fail — was silent replica-write fail).
- Surgical edits only — change what the prompt asks and nothing more; diagnose before you touch.
- Verify after every change — build, restart, hit the route — before reporting it done.
- `HUB_REDESIGN_*` and other `auto_config` flags are Ghost-controlled rollout switches — never flip one without explicit direction.
- **Write-gateway enable flags (W-C-P2a, 2026-06-06) are `auto_config` rows too, default OFF (absent or != 'true'):** `HUB_CAPITAL_WRITE_ENABLED` (capital + pnl-stats writes) · `HUB_TRADE_EDIT_ENABLED` (margin/entry edits, flip/close/delete, dca) · `HUB_LIST_WRITE_ENABLED` (quality + watchlist) · **`HUB_BENIGN_WRITE_ENABLED`** (W-C-P2b benign writes: trade notes, reminders, chat logs, journal gen — Hub-wired 2026-06-07, VM op-map pending). The **VM gateway** enforces them authoritatively (`SELECT value FROM auto_config`); a 423 means "locked — flip the row to enable". `killswitch` is ungated (emergency stop). Same governance: never flip without explicit direction.
- **Brain-file edits** go through `/api/memory/brain/[name]` → `write_brain_file.py`, gated by the `HUB_BRAIN_EDIT_ENABLED` flag; the bot's protected brain documents are rejected with HTTP 423.
- **Secrets**: `.env.local` (perms 600) holds `DASHBOARD_USER` / `DASHBOARD_PASS`; `.env` holds `DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN`. Append-only — never overwrite an existing key. `/api/auth` re-reads `.env.local` on every POST, so a password rotation needs no restart.
- **Never modify files under `/home/trevor/trevor/`** from a Hub prompt — only read its `trevor.db`.
- Bot-side engineering rules — file protection, deploy discipline, the honesty protocol — live in `/home/trevor/trevor/BEHAVIOR_RULES.md` + `/home/trevor/trevor/CLAUDE.md`. Cross-reference them; do not copy them here.
- Honesty protocol applies to Hub work too: never claim done / fixed / working without pasted command output.

### 2026-06-11 — SH-HUB: Shadow page — kill the misleading +$743 would-have-PnL artifact + dynamic enumeration + divergent-n/promotion/freshness — DEPLOYED
> Build code SH-HUB. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** (`ghost@Ghost`) at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`, port **3000**. Reshapes the Intel→Shadow page (`<ShadowOverview>`). **Display-only, READ-ONLY over the WSL litestream replica (`mode=ro`); no bot/live-DB/`auto_config` write, no `hyperliquid` import, no trading-behavior change.** Data path: `query_*.py` hardcode `/home/trevor/trevor/trevor.db`, which on this box is a **symlink → `/home/ghost/trevor-replica/trevor.db`** (the replica) — so reads hit the replica with no env override needed.
> - **🛑 THE $743 FIX (the whole reason for the prompt).** `partial-shadow-card.tsx` rendered a green `MetricTile "Would-have P&L 7d"` = `would_have_profit_usd_7d`, computed in `query_partial_shadow.py` as the **SUM of `notes_json.profit_usd` across EVERY per-cycle `would_fire=1` row** in `partial_trigger_shadow`. The bot writes one row **per monitor cycle per trade**, so each trade's slice was re-counted ~40–70× → a hugely inflated "+$X profit" that was **not realizable money**. **Measured on the live replica (7d):** raw SUM ≈ $575.88 today (Ghost saw $743.48 at recon — it floats with the window/data) across **1,534 fired-rows / 37 distinct trades**; honest de-duped realizable (one partial / distinct trade) ≈ **$8.95**. **Ghost ruling at the THOUGHTS gate: REMOVE the dollar entirely, show honest COUNTS** — Option 2 (banked from `live_partial_shadow`) was proven non-viable (its `executed=1` rows carry no `profit_usd`), and even the de-duped ~$8.95 is a fragile per-cycle counterfactual on a live feature. The green tile is **gone**; replaced by **distinct-TRADE counts** (`would_fire_trades_7d/24h` via `COUNT(DISTINCT auto_trade_id)`) + near-miss + total-evals tiles, with an explicit caveat line ("Counts are distinct trades… eval-rows run ~40–70× higher… the per-cycle profit sum was a counting artifact, not realizable money"). `by_ticker` likewise drops the dollar → shows distinct would-fire trades + eval-rows. The per-row `profit_usd` in the Recent list and the now-dead `fmtMoney` helper were also removed (no green money anywhere). `would_have_profit_usd_7d` is deleted from the Python payload, the TS interface, and the route fallback.
> - **DYNAMIC ENUMERATION (`query_shadow_registry.py` rewrite — the biggest gap).** The registry was a **hardcoded 31-entry `TABLE_DEFS`**; the replica actually has **49 shadow-named tables (+3 audit adjuncts = 52)**, so **~18 SHADOW-RM roadmap tables were 100% invisible** (direction_gate, vol_adaptive_leverage, risk_budget_account_snapshot / risk_budget_alt, exit_attribution, slippage_model_vs_realized, hard_stop_level_alt, per_ticker_leverage_cap, the *_altparam sweeps, entry_threshold_regime, major/weak_ticker_threshold, scan_cadence/execution_timing, cooldown_alt_window, counter_trend_penalty). Now **enumerated from `sqlite_master`** (`name LIKE '%shadow%'` ∪ curated adjuncts), with the old `TABLE_DEFS` converted to an `OVERRIDE` **metadata map** (curated display / function / ts_col / ts_kind / expected_active / **retired**). Unknown tables auto-derive: prettified display, ordered **name-keyword function heuristic** (→ "Other" fallback), best-available **ts-col priority pick** (`created_at`>`ts`>`timestamp`>`cycle_timestamp`) with unix-vs-iso detection by column type. New 0-row tables default `expected_active=False` → classify **DORMANT, not a false BROKEN**. **New roadmap tables now appear automatically with ZERO code change here.** The 2 P06-retired tables (`leverage_v2_shadow`, `exit_engine_shadow`) re-appear marked **retired** (override `retired=True`, expected_active=False) — visible-but-honest in the Dormant tab, not active/stale. **Live: 52 tables, 45 ACTIVE / 7 DORMANT / 0 BROKEN.**
> - **Per-shadow glance fields: divergent-n · %-divergence · promotion-readiness.** Registry detects a boolean divergence/would-fire column per table (`divergent` > `would_block` > `would_fire` > `would_fire_v1`); when present, computes `divergent_n` + `divergence_pct`; else honest `null`/"n/a" (no fabricated universal signal). **Promotion-readiness** = `accruing N/30` until **n≥30 divergent samples**, then **`ready`** iff the **Wilson 95% lower bound of the divergence rate excludes the no-divergence null** (closed-form, no scipy). `<CompactShadowCard>` (the only consumer) gained optional props rendering divergent-n + %div inline + a promotion `<Pill>` (READY mint / N/30 gold) in the collapsed row and a divergence/promotion block when expanded. **Live: 15 promotion-ready.**
> - **Promotion-ready surfaced at top + freshness badge.** `<ShadowOverview>` Active tab now leads with a highlighted **🎯 Promotion-Ready** `CollapsibleSection` (ready shadows pulled out of their function group, most-divergent first; the rest stay function-grouped below), then the function blocks (now incl. an **"Other"** group for unmapped tables). A top-of-page **freshness badge** reads `os.stat(realpath(DB)).st_mtime` → "replica Xm ago · refreshes ~15 min" (atomic-mv restore stamp; live ≈19 min) + "{N} shadows · {M} promotion-ready". ### 2026-06-08 — W-F-P3: Hub `trevor.running` derived from the Observatory heartbeat (OFFLINE-banner bug fixed) — DEPLOYED
> Build code W-F-P3. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** (`ghost@Ghost`) at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`. Fixes the permanent OFFLINE banner on trevor-prime.com. **WSL/Hub-only — the VM bot + `trevor.service` were NOT touched; deploy restarted `trevor-dashboard.service` only.**
> - **Root cause (W-F-P2 recon, re-verified live):** `src/app/api/status/route.ts` `computeStatus()` computed `trevor.running` by running `systemctl show trevor --property=MainPID --value` **locally on the WSL box** — but the bot unit (`trevor.service`) only exists on the VM. Live proof: `systemctl status trevor.service` → *"Unit trevor.service could not be found"*; the `show` query returns MainPID `0` → `parseInt→0` → `running:false`, **always**, since the W-D migration made WSL the Hub primary. The 60s SWR cache made the false value sticky. The DB half (`signals.total` from the local replica via the Python bridge) already worked and was left alone.
> - **The fix (one file, `route.ts`):** replaced the local-systemctl block with `resolveBotRunning(serviceName)` — a `fetch` of the Observatory heartbeat the Hub **already proxies** for `account_value_usd` (`https://trevor-prime.tail068f72.ts.net:8443/api/heartbeat`, same VM endpoint + same `fetch`/`AbortSignal.timeout(5000)` idiom as `src/app/api/auto/state/route.ts` — **no new VM dependency, no new fetch mechanism**). Dropped the now-unused `runCommand` import. Response shape byte-identical (`trevor: { running, pid }`), so the 3 banner consumers (`header.tsx:84`, `status-bar.tsx:30`, `header-legacy.tsx:70/80/82/86`) needed **no edits**.
> - **Derivation rule:** `running = heartbeat HTTP 200 AND timestamp parseable AND (now − timestamp) < 3h AND the services.items entry named "trevor"(.service) has active===true`. `pid = parseInt(entry.pid) || 0` (improves the legacy PID display). The service-name match strips a trailing `.service` so env `TREVOR_SERVICE_NAME=trevor` matches the heartbeat's `"trevor.service"`. **Gated on the entry's `active` flag (mirrors the VM `systemctl is-active`), NEVER on `overall_status`** — that's a separate, independently-flapping health axis (recon saw `critical`; live now `ok`).
> - **Freshness threshold N = 3h (1.5× the 2h heartbeat cadence).** `heartbeat-view.tsx:208` `HEARTBEAT_CADENCE_SECONDS=7200` → the snapshot republishes every 2h, so its age ranges 0–7200s in normal operation. A threshold tighter than 7200s would false-OFFLINE during the back half of every window (the exact bug being killed). 3h = a full window + 1h grace before declaring the Observatory stale; bot death is still caught fast by the next publish capturing `active:false` + the VM watchdog.
> - **Fail-safe:** heartbeat unreachable / non-200 / fetch timeout / **stale (>3h)** / missing-or-inactive trevor entry → `{running:false, pid:0}` (honest OFFLINE — the Hub genuinely can't confirm the bot). The helper catches internally → never throws → can't 500 the route; the GET catch stays as belt-and-suspenders.
> - **Verification (honesty protocol):** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (`/api/status` 328 B). Throwaway fail-safe harness (verbatim helper logic) **7/7 PASS** — fresh+active→`{true,1717651}`, fresh+inactive→false, stale-4h+active→false, missing-entry→false, no-timestamp→false, http-500→false, **genuinely-unreachable URL→false (no crash)**; deleted after. Live-heartbeat proof (real bot up): `resolveBotRunning('trevor')` → `{running:true, pid:1717651}` — the field flips **false→true**. Served `/api/status` (authed) on local `:3000` AND public `trevor-prime.com` → `trevor.running:true` (see deploy confirmation). DB half unchanged (`signals.total` intact). Env untouched (only `TREVOR_SERVICE_NAME`+`TREVOR_DB_PATH` consumed; heartbeat URL hardcoded to match `auto/state`). 60s SWR cache kept (now caches a CORRECT value; a transient blip self-heals within ≤60–90s, takes no action — not a harmful sticky-false risk at the 2h heartbeat cadence).
> - **5-bug canary (all bot-side, none reachable by a read-only Hub status route):** (1) signal dedup/cooldown — untouched; (2) HOLD deleting trade cards — untouched; (3) orphaned reminders — untouched; (4) reply handler post-deploy — untouched; (5) results auto-delete — untouched.
> - **SEPARATE open item (NOT fixed here):** the heartbeat's `overall_status` health axis (recon saw `critical`, currently `ok`) is its own investigation — deliberately NOT wired into `running`.
> - **Files (2):** `src/app/api/status/route.ts` (−`runCommand` import, −13-line systecmtl block, +`resolveBotRunning` helper) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `.env*`/`tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged. Commit `fix: derive Hub trevor.running from Observatory heartbeat instead of impossible local systemctl check (W-F-P3)` on `master`.

### 2026-06-07 — RM-WSL W-E-P1: gateway watchdog INSTALLED + heal-proven live (self-healing gateway) — COMPLETE (alert deferred to W-E-P3)
> Build code E-P1. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** (`ghost@Ghost`, tailnet `100.125.247.52`) at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`, no worktree. Now that the W-D cutover made the WSL Hub the **only/primary** dashboard, the gateway needs auto-restart authority. This **installs + enables** the `trevor-gateway-watchdog.service` that W-C authored-but-deliberately-did-not-install, and **proves it heals a wedge live**.
> - **No code change needed — the authored unit was already correct.** `deploy/systemd/wsl/trevor-gateway-watchdog.service` already pointed `HEALTH_URL=http://127.0.0.1:3939/healthz` (the **unauthenticated** liveness probe, 200 `{"status":"ok"}`) — NOT the authenticated `/gateway/health` (401). So **no HEALTH_URL fix / no unit edit / no new code commit** this prompt — install only. The unit was committed in the W-C authoring pass; the ONLY repo change here is this CLAUDE.md entry.
> - **Install (the whole change):** `sudo cp deploy/systemd/wsl/trevor-gateway-watchdog.service /etc/systemd/system/` → `daemon-reload` → `enable --now`. Symlinked into `multi-user.target.wants/` → **reboot-persistent** (`is-enabled`=enabled, `WantedBy=multi-user.target`). Idle on a healthy gateway (start line `url=…/healthz interval=15s … fail_threshold=2`, then silence = healthy polls taking no action), mem ~6.6M / 64M cap.
> - **Mechanism (reuses `deploy/scripts/dashboard_health_watchdog.sh` VERBATIM, parameterized via `Environment=`):** `HEALTH_URL=:3939/healthz`, `WATCHDOG_SERVICE=trevor-gateway.service`, `WATCHDOG_STATE_DIR=/home/ghost/.local/state/trevor-gateway-watchdog` (gateway-specific — never collides with the dashboard watchdog's state), `ENV_FILE=…/.env.local`, `User=ghost`, `Restart=always`, `MemoryMax=64M`. `FAIL_THRESHOLD=2` consecutive `/healthz` misses (poll every 15s) → `sudo systemctl restart trevor-gateway.service` (ghost has NOPASSWD sudo); `RESTART_COOLDOWN=300s`; windowed give-up `3/30min` → alert-only. The shared script is **NOT edited** (the VM dashboard watchdog uses it too).
> - **HEAL PROVEN LIVE (reversible wedge test, journal-evidenced):** `12:03:43` `sudo systemctl stop trevor-gateway` → `12:03:46` probe `HTTP 000`, `failures 1/2` → `12:04:01` `2/2` → `RESTARTING trevor-gateway.service (windowed restart 1/3)` → `sudo … systemctl restart trevor-gateway.service` → `restart issued` → `12:04:16` `✅ RECOVERED — responding again (1 restart in 30min)`. **The gateway was brought back by the watchdog's hand, not mine.** End state: gateway `active`, `/healthz` 200, watchdog back to idle (`consecutive_fails=0`, `was_unhealthy=0`).
> - **⚠️ KNOWN GAP — #qa-agent Discord alert does NOT fire (DEFERRED, not a watchdog defect):** every `alert_qa` logged `WARN: DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN not found in …/.env.local — alert skipped`. Root cause: the Hub's `.env.local` has `DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN=` **empty** (same file `trevor-gateway.service` itself reads via `EnvironmentFile=`) — the Discord **bot is not on this box**, so its token lives elsewhere. The watchdog is behaving correctly (no token → skip). **Fix is NOT to paste the bot's token** (blast-radius rule — the Hub gets its OWN dedicated token). Deferred to **W-E-P3 (Hub health→Discord)**, where all Hub Discord alerting is wired once with a dedicated Hub token. **No "done on alerting" claim.**
> - **No collateral:** `/gateway/health` still 401 + `/healthz` still 200 (endpoints unchanged); `trevor-dashboard.service` active + serving (`127.0.0.1:3000/api/health` 200); the W-C write-gateway wiring + op-allowlist untouched (auth gate still enforcing = 401); **bot untouched** (not on this box). My Phase-0 audit miss (called the token "present" by matching the *key*, missed the empty *value*) is logged here so the next install audit checks for a non-empty value, not just the key.

### 2026-06-07 — RM-WSL W-D-P1: edge auth hardening — HMAC-signed expiring tokens + salt regen + Tailscale Serve (TrevorHub WSL box) — COMPLETE
> Build code D-P1. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** (`ghost@Ghost`, tailnet `100.125.247.52`) at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`, no worktree. Makes the WSL dashboard safely reachable as the **primary** ahead of the W-D-P2 cutover. **Hybrid auth** kept: tailnet membership (Serve) + the app login (cookie). VM untouched; VM dashboard NOT stopped (that's W-D-P2).
>
> **Token model hardened.** Replaced the legacy **reversible, unsigned** `base64url(user:pass:salt)` token — which the middleware "validated" by only checking `parts[2] === salt`, so anyone with the salt could forge a session — with an **HMAC-SHA256-signed, expiring** token: `base64url(JSON{u,iat,exp}) . base64url(HMAC_SHA256(key=SESSION_SALT, msg=payload))`, 7-day `exp`, verified server-side (recompute HMAC → constant-time compare → reject on bad sig OR past `exp`). Password is **no longer embedded** (the signature proves issuance; password is checked at login before signing).
> - **New shared module `src/lib/session-token.ts`** — the SOLE encode/decode authority (`signToken`/`verifyToken`), imported by both `middleware.ts` (Edge runtime) and `/api/auth/route.ts` (Node) so the two sites can't drift. Edge-safe: Web Crypto (`crypto.subtle`) + `Buffer` base64url only. All 6 call sites updated (3 encode in `/api/auth`: login, no-pass login, change-password; 2 decode: middleware + the GET `?token=` verifier; +1 salt read) — no old-format path remains (grep-clean).
> - **`SESSION_SALT` regenerated** off the dead 14-char default `trevor-mc-2025` → `openssl rand -hex 32` (64-char), in gitignored `.env.local` (never printed/committed). **Hardcoded fallback killed** at both `middleware.ts:4` and `route.ts:30`; `getSalt()` now **fails closed** (throws → verify rejects) if `SESSION_SALT` is unset or `<16` chars — no default, ever.
> - **One-time re-login (expected):** the salt + format change invalidated all existing 7-day cookies. Verified the fresh login round-trip issues a new signed cookie end-to-end.
> - **Exposure = Tailscale Serve** (kept the safer localhost bind; no rebind). `sudo tailscale serve --bg --https=443 http://127.0.0.1:3000` → **`https://trevorhub-wsl.tail068f72.ts.net/`** with a real Let's Encrypt cert (`issuer Let's Encrypt CN=YE2`, TLS verify ok), **tailnet-only** (NOT Funnel). Required enabling **HTTPS Certificates** + **Serve** in the tailnet admin console first (owner-gated; CertDomains was empty until then). Config persisted in tailscaled ipn state (`_serve/…`, `--bg`) + `tailscaled.service` enabled → survives reboot/distro-restart.
> - **Verification (all PASS, evidence-backed):** route verifier `GET /api/auth?token=` → valid `true`, tampered/expired/legacy-format all `false`; middleware gate on `/api/status` → tampered/expired/legacy/no-cookie all **401**, good signed cookie **200**; bad-cred login **401**; **write-gateway still gated** — forged/absent session → **401 before** the gateway (tested `/api/trades/close|flip|edit-entry`); `/api/health` exempt **200**, gateway `/healthz` **200**. Full hybrid round-trip over the TLS Serve URL: unauth `/` → `/login` (200), authed `/` → `/autotrader` (200). Dashboard bind unchanged `127.0.0.1:3000`; service rebuilt (`next build`, prod mode `next({dev:false})`) + restarted, active.
> - **Flagged follow-up (NOT fixed — pre-existing + out of scope):** `change-password` still doesn't invalidate previously-issued tokens. The Edge-runtime middleware is **credential-blind by design** (verifies only signature+expiry; can't read `.env.local`, and `process.env` is frozen until restart), so enforcing token invalidation on password change needs either a service-restart-on-change or a request-time shared store the middleware can read — an architecture change beyond this surgical auth rework. New change-password still issues a fresh valid signed token.
> - **W-D-P2 (next, separate prompt after VM-side recon):** cut the primary over to the WSL dashboard and retire the VM dashboard. Do NOT do it here.

### 2026-06-06 — RM-WSL W-C-P2a: write-gateway LIVE for the dangerous routes (Hub half) + brain sacred-guard fix
> Build code C-P2a. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`, no worktree. Makes the two-hop write path real for the 18 dangerous `trevor.db` writers, makes audit mandatory (VM-side), and fixes the `/api/brain` sacred-guard `.md` bug. **The VM-side gateway (Phase 1) is NOT built here — no VM shell this session; it is deferred to a separate VM-SSH'd paste. Until it lands, every gated write fails CLOSED with 502/504 (the correct safe state — no write is possible).**
>
> **Architecture (two-hop, op-allowlist).** Hub route → local gateway (`127.0.0.1:3939`, bearer `GATEWAY_TOKEN`) → Tailscale → VM gateway (to be built) → bot helper → live `trevor.db`. The client never sends a helper path or SQL — only an **op name** from a fixed allowlist + input-validated args. Reads are untouched (still hit the local `0444` replica directly).
> - **`gateway/ops.js` (new):** zero-dep op-allowlist registry (19 ops) — `op → {flag, validate(args)}`. Hub-side validation mirrors each route's existing checks; rejects unknown ops (400) + bad args (400) before forwarding.
> - **`gateway/server.js` (was the skeleton):** added `POST /gateway/write` at the old placeholder. Bearer-auth (reused), parse + validate the op envelope `{op,args,actor,source_type,reason,idempotency_key}`, then forward over Tailscale to the VM (`VM_GATEWAY_IP:VM_GATEWAY_PORT`, bearer `VM_GATEWAY_TOKEN`). Timeout→504, conn-error/VM-5xx→502, else pass through 200/423/400/401. **Holds NO DB connection — still zero Hub-side writes.** `GET /gateway/health` now reports `gateway:"live", writes_enabled:<vm-configured>`.
> - **`src/lib/gateway-client.ts` (new):** `callGateway(op,args,opts)` / `gatewayWrite(...)` — the route side. POSTs to the local gateway with a fresh `idempotency_key`, normalizes fail modes (423 locked / 502 unreachable / 504 timeout / 400 validation), unwraps the helper `result` on 200 so client contracts stay byte-compatible.
> - **18 routes rewired** to call the gateway instead of writing the read-only replica: `capital` · `admin/reset-capital` · `admin/reset-pnl-stats` · `trades` PATCH (the inline `UPDATE active_trades SET margin_usd` SQL is **removed** — no raw SQL leaves the Hub) · `trades` DELETE (single + bulk, one op + mode arg) · `trades/edit-entry` (Discord card edit preserved off the helper result) · `trades/flip` · `trades/close` · `dca` · `auto/config-full/[key]` · `auto/control-full/[key]` · `auto/exit-controls` · `auto/partials-toggle` · `killswitch` · `memory/autotrader-toggle` · `memory/aggressive` · `quality/[id]` · `watchlist` (POST+DELETE). Each keeps its existing route-level input validation; reads/GETs untouched.
> - **3 new write-enable flags = `auto_config` DB rows** (Ghost ruling 2026-06-06 — overrides the prompt's "VM env" wording to match the established flag convention): **`HUB_CAPITAL_WRITE_ENABLED`** (capital.set/reset, pnl_stats.reset) · **`HUB_TRADE_EDIT_ENABLED`** (trades.patch_margin/edit_entry/flip/close/delete, dca.set) · **`HUB_LIST_WRITE_ENABLED`** (quality.set, watchlist.add/remove). **Default OFF = row absent or != 'true'.** Read live by the VM gateway via `SELECT value FROM auto_config WHERE key=?` (hot-flip, no restart, control-panel-visible) — exactly like `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED`, the toggle flags, and `HUB_BRAIN_EDIT_ENABLED`. The already-gated routes KEEP their existing flags (the VM re-checks via the helper's exit-3). **`killswitch` is intentionally ungated** (emergency stop — always actionable).
> - **Audit + idempotency (VM-side, deferred):** the VM gateway will write a mandatory `change_log` row via the existing `audit_logger` (fail-CLOSED for 🔴 ops) before dispatch, and record `idempotency_key`s in a new `gateway_idempotency` table (`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` — the ONLY permitted schema add, additive). Not created this session (no VM shell).
> - **Brain sacred-guard FIX (`/api/brain`):** the old guard compared `name.toUpperCase()` against suffix-less entries, so the real filename `IDENTITY.md` → `IDENTITY.MD` **missed** the guard and could overwrite a sacred file. Now normalized (strip `.md`, any case) → hard **423**, and the `confirm_sacred` override is **removed entirely** (sacred files inviolable, matching the robust `/api/memory/brain/[name]`). `manage_brain.py` `write_file` hardened with the same sacred check + a `HUB_BRAIN_EDIT_ENABLED` auto_config gate (exit 3 → 423), defense in depth. **No `brain/*.md` file touched** (none exist on the Hub anyway); the robust sibling + `write_brain_file.py` untouched.
> - **Env (gitignored `.env.local`, append-only, values never printed):** added `VM_GATEWAY_TOKEN` (`openssl rand -hex 32` — **the VM gateway MUST use the identical value**), `VM_GATEWAY_PORT=3940`, `VM_GATEWAY_IP=100.93.113.117` (VM **tailnet** IP — deliberately separate from `HUB_VM_IP`, which `middleware.ts` uses for the VM's *public* host).
> - **Verification (Hub half, all PASS — honesty protocol):** gateway `node --check` clean; `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0. Gateway: no-token→401, unknown-op→400, bad-args→400 (validation), valid-op (VM down)→**502 `vm_gateway_unreachable` in 0.27s** (Tailscale forward genuinely fired + got refused — no hang, no non-tailnet fallback). Authenticated routes after restart: `capital`/`watchlist`/`trades`DELETE/`killswitch`/`aggressive`/`control-full` all → **502 fail-closed (NOT a 500 / readonly-DB error)**; route input-validation still 400 (bad ticker). Brain: `IDENTITY.md`→423, `identity`→423, `SOUL`→423, non-sacred+flag-off→423 (helper exit 3); helper guard fires BEFORE any write (exit 3 proven standalone). `/api/health` **200** throughout; `trevor.service` (bot, on the VM) **never touched**; `trevor-gateway` + `trevor-dashboard` restarted clean (gate-approved).
> - **DEFERRED to the VM-SSH'd paste (Phase 1 + VM half of Phase 5):** build `gateway/vm_gateway.js` (Node, tailnet-only, bearer) + the op→helper map + server-side flag check (auto_config) + mandatory `change_log` audit + `gateway_idempotency` table + `trevor-vm-gateway.service`; create the 3 new `auto_config` flag rows (default OFF); then the **423-from-VM** proof + one benign **ON** round-trip (watchlist add/remove → 200 + `change_log` + idempotency rows). Also: the Tailscale ACL lock (if not yet authored) and the 4 benign C-P2b writers + 2 GET-path budget writers.
> - **Files:** `gateway/ops.js` (new) · `gateway/server.js` · `src/lib/gateway-client.ts` (new) · 17 route files (18 ops) · `src/app/api/brain/route.ts` · `manage_brain.py` · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `.env.local` modified (append-only) but **NOT staged**.

### 2026-06-06 — W-C-P2-DELIVERY: report-delivery pipeline ported to the WSL hub (#downloads via env webhook + DOCS surfacing)
> Build code C-P2-DELIVERY. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`, no worktree. Mirrors the VM-side delivery pipeline so recon/audit reports generated on WSL post to Discord **#downloads** AND surface in the Hub DOCS zone. **WSL-local only — no VM touch, no bot touch, no trevor.db write.**
>
> - **Webhook is env-only (hard separation).** The hub uses its OWN webhook read from `.env.local` as `HUB_DOWNLOADS_WEBHOOK_URL` — never hardcoded, never printed, no fallback to the bot webhook/`DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN`. Missing var → `post_file_sync()` raises loudly (verified). `#downloads` channel id `1492922559019225261`.
> - **Delivery script (new):** `scripts/discord_file_delivery.py` — `post_file_sync(filepath, title=None, description=None, category=None)`. Reads the webhook from `.env.local` (manual parse, fail-loud), POSTs the file multipart with `?wait=true` to capture the Discord message id, then registers it via the **real `download_manager.save_download()`** (exact schema, additive). Handles file-not-found, webhook 4xx/5xx (surfaces status, no crash), and timeout (30s). No new deps — `requests` already present; `loguru`/`tz_utils` absent on WSL (stubbed/fallback, see below).
> - **`download_manager.py` ported from the VM into the repo root.** Two surgical, schema-preserving changes only: (1) `loguru` import wrapped in a try/except stdlib-logging fallback (loguru not installed on WSL; mirrors the existing `tz_utils` fallback); (2) the VM-hardcoded `DOWNLOADS_DIR` (`/home/trevor/trevor/downloads`) + delete-log path repointed to **repo-relative, env-overridable** (`HUB_DOWNLOADS_DIR` / `HUB_DOWNLOADS_LOG`, default `<repo>/downloads` and `<repo>/logs/...`). The manifest/categories schema + all read-modify-write logic are **byte-identical** to the VM. ⚠️ The bot-delete path (`_delete_discord_message`) still loads `.env` (absent on WSL) → its Discord cleanup fails-open; not in scope (delivery doesn't use it; DOCS delete relies on a `DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN` that is a placeholder here).
> - **DOCS read path repointed.** `query_downloads.py` + `query_docs_categories.py` previously hardcoded `sys.path.insert(0, "/home/trevor/trevor")` (where only the 0444 replica lives) → the DOCS zone listed nothing on WSL (ImportError → REL-04 fail-safe). Both now insert the script's own dir (the repo root, where `download_manager.py` was ported) + add `import os`. The DOCS downloads listing works on WSL again.
> - **Downloads tree:** created `downloads/{active,archive}/` at the dashboard-read path with seeded empty `manifest.json`/`categories.json` (via the module's own functions, so schema matches). `.gitignore` extended: commit the `.gitkeep` scaffolding, **ignore runtime data** (delivered files + the mutated `manifest.json`/`categories.json` — `download_manager` recreates empty ones on demand; avoids git churn + keeps delivered data/secrets out of git).
> - **End-to-end verified (honesty protocol).** Test post of the real recon report `/home/ghost/W-C-P2-recon.md` → **webhook HTTP 200** (message id `1512951982543999038`); manifest went `[]` → 1 entry (additive); authed `GET /api/intel/downloads` lists `2026-06-06_w-c-p2-recon.md` (`active_count:1`); read path `/api/health` stayed **200** throughout. Ghost visually confirms the file in #downloads. Phase-1 gates: import clean under system python (loguru stub exercised), missing-webhook path raises a clear `RuntimeError`, no `/home/trevor/trevor` functional path remains. (Known pre-existing quirk, not introduced: the TS route returns `discord_msg_id` as a JS number → precision loss past 2^53; `manifest.json` holds the correct int.)
> - **Files:** `scripts/discord_file_delivery.py` (new) · `download_manager.py` (ported, +loguru fallback + path repoint) · `query_downloads.py` + `query_docs_categories.py` (sys.path repoint + `import os`) · `.gitignore` (downloads runtime rules) · `downloads/active/.gitkeep` + `downloads/archive/.gitkeep` (scaffolding) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `.env.local` NOT committed; no webhook URL in the diff.
> - **Preference (2026-06-06):** WSL hub report delivery = `scripts/discord_file_delivery.py` `post_file_sync()` → #downloads via **`HUB_DOWNLOADS_WEBHOOK_URL` (env-only, fail-loud, no fallback)** + `download_manager.save_download()` for the manifest. `download_manager.py` now lives in the **dashboard repo root** (repo-relative `downloads/` tree, env-overridable via `HUB_DOWNLOADS_DIR`); `query_downloads.py`/`query_docs_categories.py` import it from the repo, NOT `/home/trevor/trevor`. Downloads runtime data (delivered files + manifest/categories) is gitignored; only the `.gitkeep` scaffolding is committed.

> Build codes C-P1-MESH, C-P1-GW-SKEL. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard` (NOT the VM). >

NAT mode (`eth0 172.27.110.9/20`, gw `172.27.96.1`); Windows host owns its own separate Tailscale. **This distro runs its OWN tailscaled.** `/dev/net/tun` present → **kernel networking mode** (no userspace-networking needed). Dashboard here runs on **:3000** (`.env.local` `PORT=3000` overrides server.js' 3333 default), bound `127.0.0.1`, started detached via `node server.js` (no systemd unit on this box yet — it dies on session exit; restarted this session). >
> - **Phase 1 (DONE):** Tailscale **1.98.4** installed via official script; `tailscaled` runs under **systemd** (`systemctl enable --now tailscaled`, active, pid-managed, survives reboot) in **kernel mode** — `tailscale0` TUN interface created (NOT `--tun=userspace-networking`). Userspace-networking remains the documented fallback if the mesh ever fails. No `tailscale up` in Phase 1.
> - **Phase 2 (DONE):** `tailscale up --hostname=trevorhub-wsl`, auth URL approved by Ghost in browser. This box is now a tailnet peer — **TrevorHub tailnet IP = `100.125.247.52`** (`trevorhub-wsl`). `tailscale status` lists both `trevorhub-wsl` and `trevor-prime` (`100.93.113.117`). **Mesh reachability CONFIRMED:** `tailscale ping 100.93.113.117` → pong (≈67ms **via DERP relay**; "direct connection not established" — NAT/WSL routes over the DERP relay, functional, slightly higher latency than a direct WireGuard path; direct may negotiate later). `curl :80` on the VM → 000 (closed, **expected** — the VM gateway is C-P2's job; this only proves L3 routing). **No DB connection, no write, no auth attempted against the VM.**
> - **Phase 3 (DONE):** Write-gateway **skeleton** at **`gateway/server.js`** — a **standalone Node `http` process** (zero deps), bound `127.0.0.1:`**`3939`** (`GATEWAY_PORT`-configurable), deliberately separate from the dashboard process so it can't disturb :3000. **Machine-to-machine bearer auth** (`GATEWAY_TOKEN`, fresh `openssl rand -hex 32`, in gitignored `.env.local`, never printed/committed; constant-time compare) — SEPARATE from the dashboard's `trevor_session` cookie auth. Routes: `GET /gateway/health` → `{"status":"ok","gateway":"skeleton","writes_enabled":false}` (200 w/ valid bearer), 401 without/with bad token, 404 else; every path wrapped so a handler can't crash the process. **ZERO DB connection / ZERO write logic** — only a marked `// W-C-P2 WILL ADD WRITE ROUTES HERE` block. Started detached (`node gateway/server.js`); not yet a systemd unit (the production gateway runs ON the VM in C-P2). **Verification gate (all PASS):** no-token→401, bad-token→401, valid-token→200+`writes_enabled:false`, unknown-path→404, **dashboard `/api/health` still 200** (skeleton didn't disrupt reads). `.env.local` confirmed gitignored (`.gitignore:10`).
> - **Phase 5 / durability (DONE, 2026-06-06):** closed the gap that left the read path down at Phase 0 (services were session-children that died on session exit). Two **system** systemd units now own both processes on this WSL box (`User=ghost`, `Restart=on-failure`, `EnvironmentFile=/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard/.env.local`, `WantedBy=multi-user.target` → **survive WSL reboot**): **`trevor-dashboard.service`** (`/usr/bin/node server.js`; `PORT=3000` supplied by the EnvironmentFile) and **`trevor-gateway.service`** (`/usr/bin/node gateway/server.js`). Both `enabled` + `active`; post-`daemon-reload` health re-verified: dashboard `/api/health` → 200, gateway `/gateway/health` → 200 (auth) / 401 (no auth). Unit files are version-controlled at **`deploy/systemd/wsl/`** (kept separate from the VM's `User=trevor` units in `deploy/systemd/`; the installed `/etc/systemd/system/` copies are byte-identical). **NOTE for future sessions:** the dashboard + gateway are now systemd-managed — `sudo systemctl {status,restart} trevor-dashboard trevor-gateway`, NOT detached `node` — don't hand-start them.
