### 2026-06-02 — REL-06 + REL-08: watchdog persistent windowed give-up + restart-authority consolidation — WORKTREE, NOT MERGED
- **RM-HUB Wave 3, batch 1.** Two cohesive fixes to the Hub health watchdog's recovery machinery (`/home/trevor/scripts/dashboard_health_watchdog.sh` — standalone, NOT in either git repo). **No money path** — monitoring/recovery only; no order/size/leverage/cap/killswitch/sacred-file touch. Worktree `/home/trevor/worktrees/REL-06-08` on `feat/REL-06-08`. **No restart/daemon-reload/push inside the worktree** — the watchdog `.sh`/`.service` are *staged* under `deploy/scripts/` + `deploy/systemd/` and copied to `/home/trevor/scripts/` at the Merge Step; the bot-repo `disk_cleanup.sh` change is staged as a *patch* for Ghost to apply (see Merge Step).
- **REL-06 — persistent WINDOWED give-up counter (fixes the defeated give-up).** Pre-fix bug: `iterate()` reset `restart_cycles=0` on **any** successful health check, so a transient recovery between failures reset the give-up counter → the `MAX_RESTART_CYCLES=3` give-up never fired (a recover/wedge/recover/wedge thrash restarted forever). Doubly defeated: the watchdog service is `Restart=always`, so a watchdog-process bounce reset the in-memory counter too. **Fix:** restart timestamps now persist in `$STATE_DIR/restart_history` (one epoch per restart); the give-up is **windowed** — `MAX_RESTARTS_IN_WINDOW=3` restarts within `RESTART_WINDOW_SEC=1800` (30 min) → **ALERT-ONLY escalation**. A recovery **no longer clears the history** (the load-bearing fix); old restarts age out of the window naturally so protection auto-resumes after a genuine recovery (deliberate slow-retry, not infinite thrash, and each give-up re-pages). Escalation **loudly pages Ghost** in `#qa-agent` (🚨 "MANUAL INTERVENTION NEEDED … NOT restarting further") rate-limited by `ALERT_ONLY_REALERT` — never silently stops protecting, never silently thrashes. All counters (`consecutive_fails`, `was_unhealthy`, give-up history) are now file-backed → survive the watchdog's own `Restart=always` bounce.
- **REL-08 — single restart authority (no more restart fights).** Audit finding: only **two** actors actually issue `systemctl restart trevor-dashboard` — the watchdog and `disk_cleanup.sh` step 15 (weekly memory-hygiene restart, Sun 1 AM). **monitor_center was verified OBSERVE-ONLY** (it reads NRestarts/PID deltas in `monitors/08_service_health.py` + `14_anomaly_detection.py`; it never restarts) → **no change, do not touch**. Consolidation: a shared stand-down marker `$STATE_DIR/restart_in_progress` (`"<epoch> <owner>"`) + an `flock` on `$STATE_DIR/restart.lock` serialize the act of restarting. Whoever restarts writes the marker; the other defers while it's fresh (`RESTART_GRACE_SEC=180`, auto-expiry prevents deadlock). The watchdog ignores its *own* marker (cooldown governs its cadence) and clears it on confirmed recovery. `disk_cleanup.sh` (bot repo) no longer calls `systemctl restart` directly — it honors+sets the same marker, deferring if the watchdog is mid-recovery, with a fallback to the historic direct restart if the state dir is unavailable (never worse than today).
- **Verification (in-house, no live service touched).** New harness `deploy/scripts/test_dashboard_watchdog.sh` drives the REAL watchdog via `--once --dry-run` + test hooks (`WATCHDOG_STATE_DIR`/`WATCHDOG_NOW`/`WATCHDOG_TEST_HEALTH`, inert in prod): **14/14 PASS** — 3 restarts with a transient recovery between EACH → history NOT reset (counts 1→2→3) + give-up fires on the 4th spell (the survives-recovery proof); aged-out window → protection resumes; loud 🚨 alert content shown; watchdog defers to a disk_cleanup marker (no fight, not counted); watchdog restarts normally as sole decider + writes its own marker; ignores its own marker. disk_cleanup block tested in isolation (stubbed sudo): **5/5 PASS** — defers on fresh watchdog marker, restarts+claims marker when idle, ignores stale marker. Combined shared-state-dir proof: disk_cleanup restart + failing watchdog probes → watchdog defers, no competing restart, not charged to give-up budget. `bash -n` clean on both scripts; patch `git apply --check` CLEAN against the live bot-repo file (no shellcheck on this host).
- **Preference (2026-06-02):** the Hub health watchdog is the **SINGLE restart authority** for `trevor-dashboard.service`. Its windowed give-up = **3 restarts / 30 min → ALERT-ONLY + loud `#qa-agent` page** (never silent). All other restart actors (currently `disk_cleanup.sh`) **coordinate through the watchdog's shared stand-down marker** rather than restarting independently. `monitor_center` is observe-only and stays so. Supersedes the consecutive-`MAX_RESTART_CYCLES` semantics of the 2026-05-29 watchdog entry (now windowed); the "watchdog is the only sanctioned auto-restart" rule is unchanged.
- **⚠️ MERGE STEP** — (a) `sudo cp /home/trevor/worktrees/REL-06-08/deploy/scripts/dashboard_health_watchdog.sh /home/trevor/scripts/dashboard_health_watchdog.sh && chmod +x /home/trevor/scripts/dashboard_health_watchdog.sh`; (b) `.service` is **identical to live** — copy is optional, only `daemon-reload` if it changed (it didn't); (c) `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard-watchdog.service` to pick up the new script (state dir `/home/trevor/scripts/.watchdog_state/` is auto-created by the script on first run); (d) **bot repo (Ghost applies, NOT this task):** `cd /home/trevor/trevor && git apply /home/trevor/worktrees/REL-06-08/deploy/patches/REL-08_disk_cleanup.patch` then `bash -n disk_cleanup.sh`. Verify after: `journalctl -u trevor-dashboard-watchdog.service -n 20` shows the new `window=…/max_in_window=…/grace=…` start line.

### 2026-06-02 — REL-04/05/07/09/10/11/13: seven reliability quick-wins (batch) — WORKTREE, NOT MERGED
- **Seven independent, single-file/surgical reliability hardening fixes batched in one worktree.** No money path (Hub reliability only; REL-10's writer is the existing trade-edit surface — lock timeout only, UPDATE logic byte-identical). Zero sacred-file / zero `/home/trevor/trevor/` touches, no DB schema change, no new npm deps. Worktree `/home/trevor/worktrees/REL-QW-BATCH` on `feat/REL-QW-BATCH` (off `131bf40`, REL-02 present). **No restart/reload/push inside the worktree** — nginx reload (REL-07) + daemon-reload (REL-13) + Hub restart all happen on main at merge-all (see Merge Step).
- **REL-04 — helper fail-safe JSON.** `query_circuit_breaker.py` + `query_downloads.py` wrapped the import + dispatch in an outer `try/except` that prints a single fail-safe JSON object + `exit(0)` (loud fail-open: the error is surfaced, never swallowed), so a throw from the bot-side module yields a parseable object instead of a non-zero exit → route 500. circuit_breaker fail-safe = `{"overall_status":"UNKNOWN","error":...}` (mirrors `circuit-breaker/route.ts`'s own catch). downloads fail-safe = `{"error":...,"files":[],"stats":{},"success":false}` (superset for list + mutation consumers). Bad-input branches keep `sys.exit(0)` (SystemExit not caught by `except Exception`). **Verified in-house** (runpy + stubbed bot modules): forced import-failure (circuit_breaker) and forced runtime-failure (downloads `list_downloads` raises) both → fail-safe JSON + exit 0; real-module happy paths still valid (`overall_status/override_active/layers`; 8 files).
- **REL-05 — abort Anthropic stream on SSE disconnect.** `chat/stream/route.ts`: added a shared `AbortController` (closure over `start`+`cancel`), threaded `{ signal: ac.signal }` into `client.messages.stream(body, options)` (confirmed `RequestOptions.signal` exists in `@anthropic-ai/sdk` — request-options.d.ts:56), and `cancel()` (fired on client disconnect) now calls `ac.abort()`. Stops upstream generation/token-billing when the client drops; the resulting AbortError lands in the existing try/catch → `controller.close()`. **Verified in-house**: mock abortable `messages.stream` + the route's start/cancel wiring — firing `ac.abort()` after 3 events halts iteration at received=3 (not 1000), stream observed the abort.
- **REL-07 — nginx `proxy_buffering off` for SSE.** Added a dedicated `location /api/chat/stream` block (longest-prefix → wins over `location /`) to `deploy/nginx/trevor-hub.conf` with `proxy_buffering off; proxy_cache off;` + SSE-friendly proxy settings, so chat tokens flush immediately instead of being held in nginx's buffer. (Prior state: SSE flowed through `location /` with no buffering directive; the route's `X-Accel-Buffering: no` header was the only mitigation. The `proxy_buffering off` that already existed at line 111 is the unrelated **n8n** block.) **Verified**: `sudo nginx -t` PASS on the proposed config via transient install→test→restore; live config md5 unchanged, **no reload**.
- **REL-09 — instant, dependency-free `/api/health`.** Removed the `fs.accessSync`/`fs.statSync` on the 974MB `trevor.db` (+ `DB_PATH`/`import fs`/`dbOk`/`dbSizeKb` + the unconsumed `database` response block). The probe is now pure CPU/memory (`os` + `perf_hooks` + `process.uptime`) — **no filesystem syscall**, so a hung FS can't hang the health check (the exact failure a health check must survive). `status = highLag ? "degraded" : "healthy"`; liveness preserved (the endpoint answering 200 *is* the signal — a wedged loop never returns 200 → watchdog restarts on the HTTP timeout; the `event_loop` p99 check stays the degraded signal). **Safe — zero body consumers**: the only `/api/health` reference in `src/` is `middleware.ts` (auth-bypass allowlist); the watchdog reads the HTTP code only; `heartbeat-view.tsx`'s `database.*` reads are from `/api/heartbeat`, not `/api/health`. The `database` field was dropped (not faked) per Ghost — honest over shape-stable. **Verified**: route is fs-free (only `next/server`/`os`/`event-loop-lag` imports; the sole `statSync`/`accessSync` strings are in the explanatory comment); degraded-logic repro (p99 320→degraded, 14→healthy).
- **REL-10 — `busy_timeout` on `PATCH /api/trades`.** One line — `conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000")` after `sqlite3.connect()` and **before** the UPDATE in the inline writer (`route.ts:137`). Waits up to 5s for a write lock instead of failing immediately when the bot holds the DB. The `UPDATE active_trades SET margin_usd=...` is byte-identical — no logic change to the trade-edit surface (no money path).
- **REL-11 — `AbortSignal.timeout` on the Discord PATCH.** Added `signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000)` to the `fetch()` in `trades/edit-entry/route.ts:61` so an unresponsive Discord API can't hang the request. AbortError lands in the existing non-fatal try/catch (DB stays the source of truth).
- **REL-13 — `TimeoutStopSec` 90→15.** Confirmed live `TimeoutStopUSec=1min 30s` (systemd default, no explicit line). Staged a drop-in `deploy/systemd/trevor-dashboard.service.d/timeout.conf` → `[Service]\nTimeoutStopSec=15` (mirrors REL-01's `.service.d` drop-in approach; coexists with `cpuweight.conf`). Faster SIGTERM→SIGKILL on stop → faster watchdog recovery (aligns with the watchdog's 15s `--max-time`).
- **Gates:** Python `py_compile` clean (both helpers); `npx tsc --noEmit` **0 errors** (whole project, via worktree-local `node_modules` symlink — gitignored, removed before commit, NOT committed); LSP clean on edited TS (the two Pyright "unresolved import" notes on the helpers are pre-existing — `circuit_breaker`/`download_manager` are bot modules the Hub analyzer can't see, imported the same way in the originals). No `npm run build` (worktree has no `.next`; build + restart happen at merge-all).
- **Regression posture:** Hub-only, no bot restart → all 5 recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) are bot-side → trivially untouched. No `auto_config`/flag flips, no design tokens, no chart code.
- **Files (8 + CLAUDE.md):** `query_circuit_breaker.py` · `query_downloads.py` · `src/app/api/chat/stream/route.ts` · `src/app/api/health/route.ts` · `src/app/api/trades/route.ts` · `src/app/api/trades/edit-entry/route.ts` · `deploy/nginx/trevor-hub.conf` (+1 SSE location block) · `deploy/systemd/trevor-dashboard.service.d/timeout.conf` (new) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` + the `node_modules` symlink NOT staged. Commit prefix `fix(hub reliability):` on `feat/REL-QW-BATCH`; not pushed/merged.
- **⚠️ MERGE STEP (on main, after merging `feat/REL-QW-BATCH`):** (a) **REL-07 nginx** — `sudo cp deploy/nginx/trevor-hub.conf /etc/nginx/sites-available/trevor-hub` → `sudo nginx -t` → `sudo systemctl reload nginx` (note: this staged file also carries PERF-01's `/_next/static` alias — coordinate with PERF-01's merge so they don't clobber); (b) **REL-13 daemon-reload** — `sudo cp deploy/systemd/trevor-dashboard.service.d/timeout.conf /etc/systemd/system/trevor-dashboard.service.d/timeout.conf` → `sudo systemctl daemon-reload`; (c) **Hub restart** — rebuild + `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service`. Then live-verify: `/api/health` instant + no `database` block; `systemctl show -p TimeoutStopSec` = 15s; chat SSE streams token-by-token; `/api/circuit-breaker` + downloads healthy.
- **Preference (2026-06-02):** Hub reliability conventions — Python API helpers wrap their main logic in an outer try/except that prints fail-safe JSON + exit 0 (loud fail-open, error surfaced) so a helper throw never becomes a route 500; `/api/health` is a dependency-free liveness probe (no filesystem syscall — the 200 itself is the signal, plus the `event_loop` p99 degraded flag); SQLite write helpers set `PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000`; outbound `fetch()` to external APIs (Discord) carry `AbortSignal.timeout(5000)`; SSE routes abort the upstream on client disconnect; `trevor-dashboard.service` `TimeoutStopSec=15` via `.service.d` drop-in.

### 2026-06-02 — REL-02: patch Next.js 15.3.3 → 15.5.19 (close critical RCE GHSA-9qr9-h5gf-34mp + postcss moderate) — DEPLOYED
- **Vendor security patch. SOLO on `master`, deploy-checklist event (build → tsc → isolated-port smoke → coordinated restart → live verify).** Closes a live **critical RCE in the React flight protocol — GHSA-9qr9-h5gf-34mp** — plus ~20 other Next.js criticals/highs/moderates bundled in the same `next` advisory group, and the next-bundled **postcss moderate** (GHSA-qx2v-qp2m-jg93). Hub-only; no money path, no bot files, no trading logic.
- **⚠️ Prompt-vs-reality correction (Phase 0 gate, Ghost-confirmed).** The prompt framed the audit as "critical RCE + a high + a moderate, all in Next.js." Live `npm audit --omit=dev` showed 3 vulns but **only 2 are in the Next.js chain**: (1) critical `next` (incl. the RCE) — closed by the bump; (2) moderate `postcss` 8.4.31 nested under `next` — NOT cleared by the bump (15.5.19 still ships postcss 8.4.31) → needed an `overrides`; (3) **high `lodash` 4.17.23 via `recharts@2.15.4`** — NOT a Next.js dep, NOT touched by this wave. Per Ghost **Option 2**: bump next + postcss override; the recharts/lodash-high gets its own dedicated prompt later (it touches every chart = real regression risk against "smallest safe change").
- **Target = `next@15.5.19`** (npm `backport` dist-tag — the security-maintained 15.x line). Smallest bump that clears the full next CVE group while staying on **15.x** (latest is 16.2.7 — major bump avoided; `15.3.9` does NOT clear the group, so npm's resolver picked 15.5.19). Re-pinned **exact** (`"15.5.19"`, matching the repo's exact-pin house style for next/react) — `npm install` had rewritten it to `^15.5.19`.
- **No `--force` / `--legacy-peer-deps` needed.** next@15.5.19 peer range `react: ^18.2.0 || ^19.0.0` — pinned `react@19.0.0` satisfies it; plain `npm install next@15.5.19` resolved cleanly. (The audit's "`--force`" was only about the exact-pin being outside semver range, NOT a peer conflict.)
- **postcss override required + confirmed.** `overrides: { "postcss": "^8.5.10" }` added to `package.json`. Without it the moderate persisted (next pins postcss 8.4.31). After the override, postcss deduped to **8.5.15** across the whole tree (next-nested copy gone), clearing the moderate. The override was NOT dropped — the before/after audit proved it was load-bearing.
- **`server.js` upgrade-break risk: none.** It uses only the stable custom-server API (`next({dev:false,dir})`, `app.getRequestHandler()`, `app.prepare()`) — unchanged across 15.x. The `event_loop` `perf_hooks` histogram (RM-DASH Fix 7) is pure Node, untouched. `next.config.ts` keys (`compress`/`poweredByHeader`/`reactStrictMode`/`redirects`/`headers`/`webpack` passthrough) all still supported in 15.5.x — no config edit, no deprecated keys.
- **Before → after `npm audit --omit=dev`:** `{1 critical (next, incl. RCE) + 1 high (lodash) + 1 moderate (postcss)}` → `{1 high (lodash, out-of-scope recharts)}`. **GHSA-9qr9-h5gf-34mp GONE; postcss moderate GONE.** The lone remaining high is the deliberately-deferred recharts/lodash item.
- **Phase 1 gate:** `npm run build` exit 0 (all routes compiled; sizes unchanged vs pre-upgrade — `/autotrader` 20.2 kB, `/intel` 12.7 kB, `/memory` 15.3 kB, `/docs` 8.66 kB, `/stocks` 5.71 kB, middleware 34.4 kB → no behavior drift). `npx tsc --noEmit` **0 errors (baseline was also 0 → no new type errors)**.
- **Phase 2 — MANDATORY smoke BEFORE prod restart (all PASS), on isolated `PORT=3999` with the live :3333 service untouched:** new build booted under `server.js` (`[HUB] ready on :3999`); `/api/health` → `healthy` + **`event_loop` telemetry present** (`mean 15.45 / p50 10.17 / p99 14.67 ms`); SSR `/login` → 200 (10761 B); auth gate `/api/status` unauth → 401; cookie login → 200; authed `/api/status` → **valid JSON data** (`running:true`, 1890 signals, recent signals); static chunk `webpack-…js` → 200 (new manifest↔disk consistent). Test server then killed; live :3333 confirmed still up throughout.
- **Phase 3 — deploy + live verify.** `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` (single coordinated restart to close the post-build stale-manifest window): PID 4170614 → 4186920, `[HUB] ready` clean, 0 boot errors. **Live:** `/api/health` green via the real path + `event_loop` telemetry live; **all 15 static assets on `/login` (chunks + CSS) return 200 — no 404 storm**; authed `/api/status` valid. **Note:** the **health watchdog** (the one sanctioned auto-restart per the 2026-05-29 entry) issued a second clean restart at 02:12:52 (PID 4186920 → 4187764, `Deactivated successfully` = graceful, not a crash) during the build/restart churn — a few of my mid-window curls landed in its stop/start gap (transient 1-byte/HTTP 000), fully recovered. Post-4187764: **9/9 healthy+stable health ticks over ~45s, PID steady, event_loop p99 14.62 ms, 0 restarts/errors** in the monitor window (monitor stopped before completion).
- **Rollback:** snapshot of `package.json` + `package-lock.json` saved to `/tmp/rel-02-rollback/` before Phase 1 (plus the pre-upgrade `.next` on disk until the rebuild). **Not used** — every gate passed.
- **Sacred manifest:** `sha256sum -c /home/trevor/trevor/.sacred_manifest.sha256` → **13/14 OK**; the one FAILED (`/home/trevor/trevor/CLAUDE.md`) is **pre-existing bot-side drift** (the bot repo shows many independently-modified files incl. the manifest itself) — **this Hub-only patch touched zero files under `/home/trevor/trevor/`**, so it is not attributable to REL-02.
- **Regression posture:** Hub-only, no bot restart → the 5 recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) are all bot-side → trivially untouched. No DB/schema/`auto_config`/flag changes, no design tokens, no recharts/chart code touched.
- **Files (4):** `package.json` (next pin + postcss override) · `package-lock.json` (resolution) · `next-env.d.ts` (auto-added typed-routes reference — a 15.5.x build artifact) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged (perpetually-modified). Commit prefix `fix(security):` on `master`.
- **Preference (2026-06-02):** Hub Next.js is pinned **exact** at **`next@15.5.19`** (React 19.0.0), with `overrides: { postcss: "^8.5.10" }` to force next's bundled postcss to the patched ≥8.5.10 line. Stay on the 15.x security-backport line (`backport` dist-tag) for security patches — do not take a major (16.x) bump without a dedicated migration prompt. The recharts→lodash high (GHSA-r5fr-rjxr-66jc / GHSA-f23m-r3pf-42rh) is a **known, deferred** out-of-scope item for a separate prompt with chart-regression verification.

### 2026-06-02 — REL-01: isolate Hub from bot CPU contention via systemd `CPUWeight` (RM-HUB Wave 1, batch 2 — THE FREEZE FIX, soft lever) — APPLIED
- **Root-cause fix for the Hub dashboard freeze.** Audit refuted the memory-leak + SSR-CPU theories and proved the cause is **CPU/scheduler starvation of the Hub's single Node event loop**: on the 2-vCPU VM under sustained CPU pressure the Hub co-resides with the bot stack, gets starved → `/api/health` stops answering → the watchdog restarts it. Pre-fix baseline: **13 starts + 11 watchdog restarts/24h**; PSI `/proc/pressure/cpu` `some avg300` ≈ 67–82, load 3–4.7 on 2 cores.
- **Change = `CPUWeight` ONLY (the SOFT proportional lever).** Applied via systemd **drop-ins** (no unit file in the Hub repo): `/etc/systemd/system/trevor-dashboard.service.d/cpuweight.conf` → `CPUWeight=300`; `/etc/systemd/system/trevor.service.d/cpuweight.conf` → `CPUWeight=100` (set explicitly so the **3:1 split is intentional**, not implicit-default). Verified live: `systemctl show` CPUWeight=300/100, and kernel `cpu.weight` = 300 (Hub) / 100 (bot).
- **Why 3:1 doesn't starve the bot:** `CPUWeight` is proportional share only among *simultaneously-runnable* tasks. A healthy Hub event loop is mostly I/O-blocked (idle), so the high weight buys **prompt scheduling of its short bursts** (so `/api/health` answers before the watchdog's 15s timeout) without continuously claiming 75% — the bot's scan loops still get the CPU whenever the Hub idles (the vast majority of the time). The bot is the live-trading process and must not be starved; this split respects that.
- **NO `CPUAffinity`/`AllowedCPUs`, NO `CPUQuota`** added (confirmed: both services still show `CPUAffinity=` empty, `CPUQuotaPerSecUSec=infinity`, `Nice=0`). The hard core-reservation (`CPUAffinity`) is **DEFERRED to RM-HUB Wave 4** and stays BLOCKED until **RM-SYS Wave 1 (bot stability) is confirmed 24h-clean (0 WatchdogSec kills)** — pinning a core away from a still-wedging bot would make the bot worse.
- **⚠️ RM-SYS Wave 1 is NOT yet deployed** (bot showed **24 SIGABRT/abort events in 5 days** + ongoing `run_scalp` DATA-TIMEOUTs at audit time) → the hard-affinity escalation remains gated.
- **Zero trading interruption:** applied via `daemon-reload` (which pushes `CPUWeight` to the live cgroups) + a restart of **only** `trevor-dashboard.service`. The bot was **NOT restarted** (MainPID `4148391` unchanged before/after). Hub booted clean (`[HUB] TREVOR Hub ready on :3333`, 0 errors), `/api/health` 3×200 in 0.02–0.08s; bot scan loop running (SCAN-STEP completing ~3s/ticker post-change).
- **Revert (seconds, no restart):** `sudo rm /etc/systemd/system/trevor-dashboard.service.d/cpuweight.conf /etc/systemd/system/trevor.service.d/cpuweight.conf && sudo systemctl daemon-reload`.
- **24h validation window OPEN (Ghost-observed, not polled).** Success = watchdog restarts → ~0 and PSI `some avg300` trending down vs the 13-starts/11-restarts baseline; **also watch the bot's DATA-TIMEOUT/SIGABRT rate — it must NOT worsen** (lifting Hub freeze-thrash may even ease overall contention). Do not claim the freeze "fixed" until the 24h window confirms; this entry claims only that CPUWeight is applied + the pressure baseline captured.
- **Preference (2026-06-02):** Hub/bot systemd CPU share is a `CPUWeight` split — **`trevor-dashboard.service` CPUWeight=300, `trevor.service` CPUWeight=100** (3:1, soft lever) as of REL-01. No `CPUAffinity`/`AllowedCPUs` and no `CPUQuota` on either service (hard core-pin is gated to RM-HUB Wave 4 behind RM-SYS bot stability). Applied via `.service.d/cpuweight.conf` drop-ins, not unit files.

### 2026-06-02 — PERF-01 + PERF-02: serve `/_next/static/` from nginx disk (immutable) + migrate 13 routes to `createSwrCache` (RM-HUB Wave 1, batch 1) — WORKTREE, NOT MERGED
- **Two ambient-pressure relievers for the Hub CPU-starvation wedge. Worktree `/home/trevor/worktrees/PERF-01-02` on branch `feat/PERF-01-02`; NO service/nginx reload, NO push — picks up at the coordinated merge-all. Hub-only, read-mostly, no money path (no order/size/leverage/cap/killswitch-write/sacred-file touched).**
- **PERF-01 — nginx serves `/_next/static/` directly from disk.** The live `/etc/nginx/sites-available/trevor-hub` was *proxying* `/_next/static/` through Node on :3333 **and** overriding the headers with `no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate` + `Pragma: no-cache` + `Expires: 0` — so every content-hashed chunk re-fetched through the Node event loop on every load (and `next.config.ts`'s `immutable` header was vetoed by nginx). New block: `location /_next/static/ { alias /home/trevor/trevor-dashboard/.next/static/; access_log off; try_files $uri =404; add_header Cache-Control "public, max-age=31536000, immutable"; }`. Direct disk serve (no Node hop), 1-yr immutable cache restored. Alias points at the exact `.next/static/` the running `node server.js` builds into → disk and the HTML's referenced hashes are always in sync (content-hashed → old/new coexist). All dynamic blocks (`/`, `/ws/terminal`, `/api/scout/`, n8n server, 80→443 redirect, IP allowlist, SSL) **byte-identical** (verified by diff: only the header comment + the one `location` block changed). **Staged at `deploy/nginx/trevor-hub.conf`** in the repo. **`sudo nginx -t` passes on the proposed config** (validated by a transient install→test→restore, NO reload; live config md5 confirmed unchanged after).
- **PERF-02 — 13 read routes migrated to the proven `createSwrCache` single-flight + SWR + concurrency-cap helper** (`src/lib/single-flight.ts`, 26/26 unit tests). Audit said "11 on old pattern"; a naive `let _cache` grep missed 5 differently-named vars (`_statusCache`, `_liveCache`, `_healthCache`, `_listCache`, `_activeTradesCache`). True scope = **11 old-pattern + 2 uncached reads** (Ghost-approved all 13). Each route's existing try/catch + fail-safe fallback stays wrapped around `swr()` → cold-failure contract byte-identical; a transient WARM failure now serves stale instead of erroring (strictly better). `concurrency: 2` on every cache; existing TTL preserved exactly. **The 13 (route — TTL — key):**
  - `status` 60s · `live` 30s · `system-health` 300s (the subprocess-spawner that triggered the original wedge) — single-key; `cached` flag + per-request `latencyMs` preserved (cached:true via `peek()` when a stored value exists, race-free).
  - `killswitch` 5s GET — **POST (write) untouched**; its cache-bust `_cache = null` → `cache.clear()` so a toggle still reflects on the very next poll.
  - `auto/profit-risk` 10s · `auto/leverage-regime` 10s — fail-safe "never 500 the panel" preserved (`peek()` serves last-good in the catch).
  - `circuit-breaker` 15s · `analytics/duration` 300s · `analytics/confidence-tiers` 300s — single-key reads.
  - `trades` 15s — **only the `scope=active` GET cache** migrated (`activeCache`); `history`/`watchlist` reads + the PUT/DELETE/PATCH **write handlers untouched**; `cached:true` preserved via peek.
  - `memory/chroma` 300s — **only `mode=list`** migrated (cold-start ~38s collapses to one); `peek`/`search` stay uncached passthrough; `X-Cache: HIT/MISS` preserved via peek.
  - `auto/trades` 15s (was UNCACHED behind a 15s poll) — keyed by `${type}:${limit}` so open/closed × each limit hold independent entries.
  - `analytics/regime-performance` 300s (was UNCACHED) — matches its analytics siblings.

(Live 200-from-disk + route smoke happen on main after the nginx reload + Hub restart in the merge step.)

- **`/api/prices` now serves the bot-side WebSocket price store on top of the existing 30s HL→CG cache.** New `tryWsPrices(tickers)` Node-fetches the Observatory aiohttp endpoint `http://127.0.0.1:3335/api/prices` (1500ms timeout) — the same localhost-proxy pattern as `/api/heartbeat`→3335. WS miss (stale >10s / unreachable / incomplete) → **falls through to the existing unchanged `pricesCache.swr(...)` + `computePrices` HL→CG chain** — so the fallback is **byte-identical to the prior contract** (same 30s cache, same per-ticker `stale`, same `{prices:{TICKER:{price,source,stale}},timestamp}` shape). The 2s poll only ever hits the WS store / existing cache — never a fresh HL REST.

### 2026-05-30 — S3-P07: Signal-Overlay Shadow Comparison panel (Intel → Promote)
- **New READ-ONLY Intel sub-tab `Promote`** (`/intel?tab=promote`) — the go/no-go decision surface for promoting Stage-2/3 shadow overlays to live. One card per overlay with sample size, would-have-helped metric, estimated P&L delta vs actual, and a **promote-readiness verdict**. **Never sends commands** — GET-only over `trevor.db` (`?mode=ro`).
- **6 overlays surfaced** (each logs its own table — the "source tag" IS the table): Regime-Aware Exits `regime_exit_shadow` (S2-P04) · Funding Signal `funding_signal_shadow` (S3-P01) · CVD/OI Hold-vs-Bank `cvd_oi_exit_shadow` (S3-P02) · Reversal Early Exit `reversal_exit_shadow` (S3-P03) · Exhaustion Entry Filter `exhaustion_entry_shadow` (S3-P04) · Time/Session Exits **(S3-P06, built in parallel — table not yet present)**.
- **Promote-readiness enum (honest, threshold-gated):** `insufficient_data` (<`MIN_SAMPLES`=20 resolved divergent decisions — **never a conclusive number below threshold**) · `not_reporting` (table missing) · `underperforms` (est P&L delta ≤0 OR helped% <50) · `candidate` (≥20 samples + positive delta + helped% ≥50). Underperformers flagged loudly (red glow); candidates green glow.
- **Honesty — panel is "insufficient_data" for all 6 at launch BY DESIGN.** 5 of the 6 tables have **0 rows** (shadows just deployed, inert until live trades resolve outcomes); the 6th (S3-P06) **doesn't exist yet** (parallel task) → `not_reporting`. Verified live: summary `{candidate:0, underperforms:0, insufficient_data:5, not_reporting:1}` — exactly the expected honest launch shape, no fabricated stats. Each overlay lights up automatically as resolved divergent decisions accumulate (60s/15s poll, no Hub change). **`time_gate_shadow` (92 rows, an Entry gate) was deliberately NOT mislabeled as the S3-P06 exit table** — S3-P06 registers a probed candidate name set (`session_exit_shadow`/`time_exit_shadow`/…), first-that-exists wins, no coupling.
- **Per-overlay metric derivation is documented + echoed in the payload (`metric_basis`)** for transparency — the overlays log heterogeneous columns: exhaustion = avoided-loss (`-eventual_outcome` for `would_block=1` rows where the blocked entry lost); cvd_oi/reversal = early-bank (`r_multiple − eventual_outcome` for divergent rows; helped when the held position gave back); funding = directional hit-rate (`outcome_confirmed`, no P&L delta — shown as "n/a"); **regime_exit = divergence-only** (its logged columns mix R and percent units with no realised-R field, so a P&L delta is honestly NOT derivable → null metric, divergence tracked only — a genuine instrumentation gap, deferred). Metric SQL validated against synthetic rows (early-bank, avoided-loss, hit-rate, and the ≥20-sample candidate threshold all assert correctly) since the live tables are empty.
- **Read pattern = S1-P06/S2-P05 extended (not forked):** new `query_shadow_compare.py` (repo root, RO sqlite `mode=ro`, per-overlay config with divergent/resolved/helped/delta SQL fragments + outer-wrap crash guard, single JSON fail-safe shape) → new `src/app/api/shadow/compare/route.ts` (`runtime=nodejs`, `force-dynamic`, `await runPython`, `safeJsonParse` FALLBACK) → `src/components/intel/shadow-compare-section.tsx` (`"use client"`, **raw fetch + 15s `setInterval`** — repo has no react-query/SWR; matches `profit-risk-panel.tsx`). **⚠️ PREFERENCE:** the S3-P07 prompt named "TanStack Query"; per Ghost the repo convention (raw fetch + poll) was followed instead — no new dependency (consistent with the S2-P05 ruling).

### 2026-05-29 — RM-PNL: Realized-only headline P&L + time windows + greyed unrealized (Auto Capital)
- **The model (Ghost-approved, intentional, non-standard):** Auto Capital headline = **REALIZED P&L only**. A closed trade's `pnl_usd` is the only thing that moves a realized total; an OPEN position contributes **$0** to every realized window regardless of floating gain/loss (its committed notional is deployed capital, not P&L). Unrealized is shown but **greyed/separate**, never summed into realized. `equity` (live HL account value) floats with open positions BY DESIGN and is labeled "live account value" so it's never confused with the booked realized number. Windows: **Today / Yesterday / 1W / 1M / All**, default Today, on **Eastern-calendar** boundaries.
- **Phase 0 audit findings:** current "Today P&L" (`query_auto_state.py` rolling-24h-UTC sum of closed-live `pnl_usd`) was already realized-only, but (a) the EQUITY headline is mark-to-market (includes unrealized) and nothing labeled it as such — the real confusion; (b) "Today" was rolling-24h-UTC, not ET-calendar; (c) no window selector, no separated unrealized line. `closed_at` is stored UTC (server localtime == UTC). The two recent `external_close` flatten rows carry realized `pnl_usd` (FARTCOIN +$0.2362, BTC −$0.0245); **5 older `external_close` rows have NULL `pnl_usd`** (never booked a number) — surfaced as `realized_unknown_count`, never invented or silently dropped.
- **Phase 1 (DONE) — backend.** `query_auto_state.py`: new `compute_windows(rows, now_utc)` (pure, importable, unit-tested) buckets closed-LIVE `(closed_at, pnl_usd)` into today/yesterday/week/month/all on ET boundaries (`_et_window_starts` via `zoneinfo America/New_York` → UTC strings; week=today−6d, month=today−29d, yesterday is the `[yday, today)` range; NULL pnl → `realized_unknown_count`, excluded from sums). `_fetch_hl_account_value` → `_fetch_hl()` returns `{equity, unrealized}` from the SAME `user_state` call (`assetPositions[].position.unrealizedPnl` summed) — no new outbound request. `open_exposure_usd` = `SUM(notional_usd)` of open rows (neutral). New response keys: `equity_usd`, `realized{}`, `realized_pct{}` (base = current equity, documented; collapses to 0 when HL down — no equity-snapshot history exists), `realized_count{}`, `realized_unknown_count`, `open_exposure_usd`, `unrealized_usd`, `open_count`, `ts`. Legacy keys preserved; `pnl_today_usd` now == `realized.today` (ET-calendar — the intended fix vs rolling-24h). `/api/auto/state/route.ts` wrapped in `createSwrCache` (10s TTL, conc 2) — it fans out to a live HL call + Python spawn (stampede shape); try/catch around `swr()` keeps the fail-safe FALLBACK contract. **Gate:** `npx tsc --noEmit` 0 errors; fixture unit test (11 rows incl. exact-boundary + NULL) → all assertions pass (ET boundaries exact, NULL→unknown, realized-only by construction); live DB run → `today=$0.1183` (n=3, both flatten rows present), `realized_unknown_count=5`, `unrealized_usd=0`/`open_count=0` (post-reconcile flat), `equity_usd` from live HL.
- **Phase 2 (DONE) — frontend.** `capital-hero.tsx` rebuilt: REALIZED headline (large green/red `MoneyText`) for the selected window + `realized_pct`; **segmented window selector reusing the `FilterChips` primitive** (Today/Yest/1W/1M/All, default Today — no duplicate primitive); a greyed secondary block (equity labeled "live account value · floats with open positions", `unrealized` rendered in flat `text-fg-faint` — deliberately NOT green/red so it never reads as booked, deployed-notional line, and `realized_unknown_count` note when >0); Trades/Open counts retained. **The labeling fix (Ghost's core need): equity = floating live account value, realized = booked — never confusable.** **Gate:** `npx tsc --noEmit` 0 + `npm run build` clean (`/autotrader` 16.2→16.7 kB); structural check — the only two colored `MoneyText` are bound to `realized`/`realized_pct`, `unrealized` appears only in greyed `text-fg-faint` blocks; binding simulation — all 5 windows resolve to distinct realized values, and an open-position-only float of +$5.42 leaves the headline at $0.00 (unmoved) with the float ONLY in the greyed line.
- **Phase 3 (DONE — Ghost-approved restart).** Commit `9d222c8` (4 files: `query_auto_state.py`, `src/app/api/auto/state/route.ts`, `src/components/autotrader-v2/capital-hero.tsx`, `CLAUDE.md` — D-wave files NOT staged). `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` → fresh uptime 12:02→13:54 UTC, new PID 499116, `[HUB] ready` in 3s, journal monitor armed across the window with **0 error/warn/exception events**. **Live `/api/auto/state`** returns the new shape (`ts` stamped): `equity_usd=$66.34`, `realized{today 0.1183, yesterday −4.0803, week −7.6365, month −15.2451, all −19.6556}`, `realized_count{3,38,97,234,270}`, `realized_unknown_count=5`, `unrealized_usd=0`, `open_exposure_usd=0`, `open_count=0`. **Independent ET re-derivation from `trevor.db` matched all 5 windows exactly.** The two `external_close` flatten rows (FARTCOIN +$0.2362, BTC −$0.0245) appear in Today realized; post-reconcile open positions show correctly (0 open, $0 exposure, $0 unrealized). New labels confirmed present in the production client bundle (`page-dac657ef…js`): "Realized P&L", "live account value", "floats with open positions", "no booked P&L". `/autotrader` HTTP 200. **Files: `query_auto_state.py`, `src/app/api/auto/state/route.ts`, `src/components/autotrader-v2/capital-hero.tsx`, `CLAUDE.md`.** Regression posture: Hub/dashboard-only, no bot restart → all bot-side recurring bugs untouched; cache-stampede fix intact (added `createSwrCache` to `/api/auto/state`, no new un-deduped route); DB read-only/additive, no schema/flag/.env changes, no new npm deps.
- **Preference (2026-05-29):** Auto Capital headline = **REALIZED P&L only** (open positions contribute $0 until closed); unrealized shown greyed/separate; windows Today/Yesterday/1W/1M/All (default Today); equity = live HL balance (labeled "live account value", floats with open positions). `realized_pct` base = current equity until a daily equity-snapshot table exists.

### 2026-05-29 — Single-flight dedup + stale-while-revalidate + concurrency cap + event-loop-lag metric (RM-DASH residual-wedge wave)
- **Root cause (per `docs/audits/RESIDUAL_WEDGE_AUDIT.md`):** the outbound-`fetch()` routes (`/api/prices`, `/api/heartbeat`) and the `runPython`-cached routes (`trade-stats`, `nav-badges`, `time-slots`, `commits`, `quality`) had a read-through cache but **no in-flight dedup and no concurrency cap** — cache written only AFTER the full miss-chain, so a synchronized poller burst at each expiry boundary launched K independent upstream chains → cache-stampede → metastable wedge (sync `JSON.parse` bursts, undici socket saturation, timer/GC pressure). The 2026-05-29 async-bridge wave (entry below) made the Python path non-blocking but left the same dedup gap (audit Fix 8). This wave implements **Fix 1 (single-flight) + Fix 2 (SWR) + Fix 3 (per-origin cap) + Fix 7 (lag metric)**. Skipped: Fix 4 (HL→CG already correctly sequential), Fix 5 (nginx edge cache — out of repo), Fix 6 (straggler timeouts — separate).
- **Phase 1 (DONE) — `src/lib/single-flight.ts` (new, ~190 lines).** `createSwrCache<T>({defaultTtl, concurrency, clock})` → `{ swr(key, compute, {ttl?}), peek, clear }`. Single-flight via `Map<key,Promise>` cleared in `.finally()`; SWR (fresh→serve / expired→serve-stale + ONE bg refresh / cold→await); in-file counting `Semaphore` (K=2 default, direct slot-handoff) for the per-origin cap; injectable clock for deterministic tests; store written ONLY on success (no error poisoning); per-route namespaced instances. **Gate:** `npx tsc --noEmit` 0 errors; compiled standalone + ran 4 unit-test categories → **26/26 assertions pass** (50-concurrent-same-key→compute ran EXACTLY once + all 50 same value; expiry→stale immediately + exactly one bg refresh, cold→awaits; semaphore caps peak concurrency at K=2 across distinct keys; throwing refresh clears in-flight + no poison + retry works, incl. 5 concurrent cold callers sharing one failing refresh).
- **Phase 2 (DONE) — `/api/prices` + `/api/heartbeat`.** `prices`: miss-chain moved into `computePrices(tickers, prev)` (never throws — HL/CG failures degrade per-ticker to prior-value-stale / zero placeholder, as before); cache key = sorted ticker set (case-sensitive — all 3 callers send the identical 10-ticker array → one in-flight/window); 30s TTL + SWR; response built in request order, shape byte-identical (`{prices:{ticker:{price,source,stale}}, timestamp}`); parse-once holds. `heartbeat`: GET wrapped in 5s-TTL single-flight with `staleWhileRevalidate:false` (await-on-expiry → each poll-tick still live, 502/503 still surface; only same-tick bursts dedupe — chosen because the 5s TTL << 30s poll cadence would make pure-SWR perpetually stale); POST untouched. **Gate:** `npx tsc --noEmit` 0 errors; `npm run build` clean (`/api/prices` builds); old-route live shape captured as baseline; standalone harness over the route's verbatim `computePrices`+cache wiring → **30 simultaneous cold-cache GETs collapse to EXACTLY 1 HL + 1 CG fetch**, all 30 responses identical, shape matches the live contract (10/10 assertions). (Helper extended with `staleWhileRevalidate` opt — re-tested 31/31.) Live autocannon fetch-once proof deferred to Phase 5 (post-restart).
- **Phase 3 (DONE) — 5 runPython-cached routes wired:** `trade-stats` (60s, `runPythonInline`, single key, fallback `{stats:{},blocked:[]}`), `nav-badges` (30s, single key, fallback `{activeTrades:0,…}`), `time-slots` (60s, single key, fallback `{slots:{}}`), `commits` (60s, `runCommand` git-log — only the load is cached; per-request filter/paginate derive stays outside; cold-fail → `{total:0,commits:[]}`), `quality` (60s, **Map-keyed by scope+args** — `summary`/`patterns_active`/`ticker_<T>`/`regime_<R>`/`recent_matches_<N>`/`by_*`; the route that genuinely needs the Map; 400 paths return before `swr()`; cold-fail → 500). Each route keeps its existing try/catch AROUND `swr()` → cold-failure contract byte-identical; SWR serves stale on a transient warm failure instead of the fallback (strictly better). **Gate:** `npx tsc --noEmit` 0 errors after all 5; `npm run build` clean (all 5 + prices + heartbeat compile); spawn-once harness over the verbatim wiring → nav-badges 40-concurrent-cold → **1 spawn**; quality 40-concurrent across 3 distinct scopes → **exactly 3 spawns (one per key)** (7/7 assertions). Live spawn-once proof deferred to Phase 5.
- **Phase 4 (DONE) — event-loop-lag metric on `/api/health` (Fix 7).** New `src/lib/event-loop-lag.ts`: process-wide `monitorEventLoopDelay({resolution:10})` histogram, enabled ONCE, stashed on `globalThis.__trevorEloopDelay`; `getEventLoopLag()` returns `{mean_ms,p50_ms,p99_ms,max_ms}` (ns→ms, NaN/sentinel→0, wrapped so it can't break the probe). `server.js` (+~10 lines, CommonJS) creates+enables the same `globalThis` histogram at startup; the lib reuses it (lazy globalThis-guarded fallback under any other entrypoint). `/api/health/route.ts` adds the additive `event_loop` key and flips `status:"degraded"` when `p99_ms > 250` (dormant signal — watchdog reads only the HTTP code). **Gate:** `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; compiled the lib standalone + ran a temp server reproducing the server.js→globalThis→lib path + verbatim route assembly → `singletonShared=true` (one histogram), real non-zero percentiles under injected lag, `degraded` flip confirmed (p99 320ms→degraded / 65ms→healthy); the ACTUAL `dashboard_health_watchdog.sh --once --dry-run` against both a 200/healthy and a 200/degraded new-shape body → NO restart action (reads only HTTP code). Production-route live curl deferred to Phase 5 (post-restart).
- **Phase 5 (DONE — Ghost-approved restart).** Committed (2 commits: `9a047c3` dedup/SWR + the lag-metric commit, this entry), `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` (clean boot, `[HUB] ready`, 0 journal errors/warns the whole test window), confirmed NEW code live (`event_loop` key + fresh uptime). **Warm smoke** all 8 routes (prices/heartbeat/health/trade-stats/nav-badges/time-slots/commits/quality) HTTP 200 in **8–25ms** (≪50ms). **Stampede-kill proof:** (1) cold-herd — 100 SIMULTANEOUS cold-cache `/api/prices` → **1 distinct response timestamp + exactly 1 `[PRICES] upstream refresh` log line** (single-flight collapse, live). (2) sustained `autocannon -c 100 -d 30` ×3 runs → **1 upstream chain per 30s window each** (with 30s TTL = ~1/window; the amplification is dead). (3) loop health during the sustained burst: event-loop **p99 ≈ 14ms, mean ≈ 12.8ms**, `/api/health` itself executing in 0–5ms and answering in 5–10ms across the whole sustained window — the wedge symptom (sustained multi-hundred-ms p99) is gone. **Honest caveat:** a one-time ~2.4s loop block at the instant 100 connections slam open (cumulative `max_ms` 1421→2401ms; 2 of 11 probe attempts timed out during the ~5s connection ramp), fully recovered by t≈7s. Only 1 upstream chain fired → this is TCP-connection-storm establishment cost on a single Next process, NOT the cache stampede and NOT metastable (recovers, doesn't amplify); it's far beyond realistic poller concurrency and the watchdog (15s timeout × 2 consecutive) does not trip. This cross-connection herd is exactly what the deferred Fix 5 (nginx edge micro-cache) absorbs at the edge. autocannon request tail latency (avg ~745ms, p99 ~2.4s, ~1% timeouts) is the single-process connection-throughput ceiling, unrelated to the (now-deduped) cache path.
- **Files (12 + this entry):** new `src/lib/single-flight.ts`, `src/lib/event-loop-lag.ts`; modified `server.js`, `src/app/api/{prices,heartbeat,trade-stats,nav-badges,time-slots,commits,quality,health}/route.ts`, `CLAUDE.md`. **Untouched on purpose:** `api-helpers.ts` (async bridge — not the wedge), the 8 in-flight D-wave files (NOT staged), every bot file under `/home/trevor/trevor/` (incl. `BEHAVIOR_RULES.md` — recorded here per Hub-no-bot-modify), sacred files, `auto_config`/DB, design tokens, client poll cadences. No new npm deps (semaphore in-file). Deferred (intentionally out of scope): Fix 4 (HL→CG already sequential), Fix 5 (nginx edge cache — out of repo), Fix 6 (straggler timeouts). **Regression canaries:** all bot-side (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD card bundling, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) untouched — this was a Hub-only, dashboard-only wave, no bot restart.
- **Helper covers both classes** (outbound-fetch + runPython) with one shape; only the `compute` body differs. Per Ghost: record status in Hub `CLAUDE.md` ONLY (NOT the bot's `BEHAVIOR_RULES.md` — Hub-no-bot-modify rule; Phase 5 task 4's BEHAVIOR_RULES reference disregarded). Watchdog confirmed body-blind (reads HTTP code only) → additive `/api/health` keys + `degraded` flip are safe + inert today.

### 2026-05-29 — Async Python bridge (fixes event-loop wedge) + health watchdog
- **Root cause of the 2026-05-29 freeze:** `src/lib/api-helpers.ts` ran every Python helper via **synchronous** `spawnSync`, which blocks the single Node event loop until the child closes its stdout pipe. A 2026-05-27 fix added `killSignal:'SIGKILL'` (kills an unkillable *direct* child) — but the loop still wedged ~8 min on 2026-05-29 because SIGKILL doesn't reap a **grandchild** that inherited the stdout pipe fd; the sync read blocks past the timeout. `query_system_health.py` (subprocess-spawner, backs `/api/system-health`) is the reachable trigger.
- **Fix — async bridge.** Rewrote `runPython`/`runPythonInline` as **async** (`spawn`, `detached:true` process group, `stdio:['pipe','pipe','pipe']` so children don't inherit our stdio, `AbortController` timeout → `process.kill(-pid,'SIGKILL')` reaps the whole group). Added `runPythonResult` (async, no-throw, exit-code→HTTP mapping for write routes) + `runCommand` (async git/tail/pgrep, no shell). A slow/hung child can **never** block the loop. Verified by a grandchild-pipe hang test: timed out in ~3 s (not 300 s), threw, and the grandchild was reaped (no orphan).
- **Scope — full conversion (~64 files committed).** Every `execSync`/`spawnSync` call site converted: ~11 raw-spawnSync write routes (exit-code mapping via `runPythonResult`), ~13 raw-execSync inline/shell routes (`status`/`system-health`/`live` dropped the dangerous `python3 -c '…escaping…'` + shell `tail`/`systemctl`/`git` → argv), and all ~63 `runPython`/`runPythonInline` callers awaited. Local sync wrappers in `chat`, `chat/stream`, `quality`, `journal` made async. Server components (`app-shell.tsx`, 4 zone `page.tsx`) awaited their feature-flag read.
- **`runPython` is now ASYNC — callers MUST `await` it.** (Supersedes the old "runPython is sync — do not await it" rule in the System State doc above; that line is stale and will be corrected on the next doc pass.)
- **Gates:** `grep -E 'execSync|spawnSync' src/` → **0** real calls (zero literal matches). `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**. `npm run build` → clean (all API routes + 5 zone pages built; `/autotrader` 16.2 kB unchanged). Service restart + live concurrent-load test are the Phase-4 deploy step (Ghost-gated).
- **Health watchdog (`/home/trevor/scripts/dashboard_health_watchdog.sh`, NOT in this repo).** systemd unit has `Restart=always` but no `WatchdogSec` → a hung-but-alive process is never restarted. The watchdog polls `/api/health` (Python-free) every 30 s (`--max-time 15`); restarts `trevor-dashboard.service` after 2 consecutive failures; no re-restart within 5 min; after 3 failed restart cycles → alert-only (no restart loop); alerts `#qa-agent` on every restart via the Bot-token REST idiom (self-contained, reads token from shared `.env`). Lives in the neutral `/home/trevor/scripts/` (respects "never modify `/home/trevor/trevor/`").
- **Preference (2026-05-29):** `trevor-dashboard.service` MAY auto-restart via the health watchdog **without per-incident approval** — the ONLY sanctioned auto-restart. All other service restarts still require explicit Ghost approval. Mitigations: rate-limit + 5-min cooldown + back-off to alert-only + alert on every restart.
- **Commit scope:** `src/lib/api-helpers.ts` + 63 converted route/page/component files + this entry. **Excluded** (in-flight D-wave work, per Ghost): `query_activity.py`, `src/app/api/auto/activity/route.ts`, `autotrader-v2/{activity,control,dashboard}-tab.tsx`, `recent-toggles-section.tsx`, `intel/shadow-overview.tsx`, `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo`. **Honesty caveat:** `activity/route.ts` carries my `await` change but stays uncommitted with the D-wave work — so a clean checkout of this commit alone would show one type error in that file until the D-wave work is committed; the running build is from the working tree (green). `npx tsc`/`npm run build` ran against my changes **plus** the in-flight D-wave edits.

### 2026-05-28 — RM-07 P02: Hub watchlist + analytics expand sacred 5 → 10 tickers
- **Frontend catch-up to the bot's P01/P01.5 sacred-ticker expansion.** The bot's `ticker_thresholds.py:TICKER_THRESHOLDS` + `auto_trader/config.py:TICKER_LEVERAGE` already carry **10** sacred tickers (`BTC, ETH, SOL, HYPE, FARTCOIN, XRP, DOGE, NEAR, SUI, kPEPE`); the Hub still hardcoded the original 5 in seven places. This wave updates every Hub-side 5-ticker list to 10.
- **⚠️ Prompt-vs-reality corrections (Phase 0 gate, Ghost-confirmed):** (1) the P02 prompt said the new set was `XRP, DOGE, CL, XAU, NEAR` + a new COMMODITY tier — **wrong**. The real sacred-10 additions are `XRP, DOGE, NEAR, SUI, kPEPE` (all crypto on HL); `CL`+`XAU` were explicitly **deferred** in the bot's P01 comment ("not on HL"), so there is **no COMMODITY tier** (tier enum stays `BLUE_CHIP | MID_CAP | MEME`). (2) The prompt's worktree setup cut from the **bot** repo (`/home/trevor/trevor`, Python); the Hub is this separate repo. Per Ghost decision this wave was done **directly on `master`** (no worktree).
- **Watchlist grid is already data-driven** — `watchlist-grid.tsx` renders `/api/auto/config → per_ticker_thresholds`, built by `query_auto_config.py` via a runtime import of the bot's `TICKER_THRESHOLDS` (now 10 keys), so the tiles auto-populate. The only Hub changes needed for the grid: (a) `query_auto_config.py:TIER_MAP` +5 entries so the new tiles get correct pill colors (`XRP/NEAR/SUI`→MID_CAP, `DOGE/kPEPE`→MEME — mirrors the bot's altcoin/memecoin tier classification; without this they'd fall through to the `"MEME"` default); (b) `WATCH_TICKERS` (price-fetch list) +5; (c) the loading skeleton `length: 5 → 10`.
- **Per-ticker colors note:** the UI has **no per-ticker color map** — pill colors derive from *tier* (`TIER_INTENT`: blue-chip→cyan-soft, mid-cap/meme→plum). Assigning correct tiers in `TIER_MAP` is the whole of the "ticker color" requirement.
- **`per-ticker-panel.tsx` (Config-tab per-ticker selector) — honest fallback handling.** Widened `SACRED_TICKERS`/`SacredTicker` to 10. The 6 source-mirror dicts split: `TICKER_THRESHOLDS` + `TICKER_LEVERAGE` have real per-ticker values for all 10 (added: XRP 38/41/44 @10x, DOGE 42/45/48 @8x, NEAR 39/42/45 @8x, SUI 39/42/45 @8x, kPEPE 42/45/48 @8x). The other four (`RECALIBRATED_THRESHOLDS_V2`, `DIRECTION_PENALTIES`, `MOMENTUM_FLOOR_BY_TICKER`, `TICKER_TRAIL_FLOORS`) **only cover the original 5** in the bot source — the new tickers use the bot's documented fallbacks (`get_direction_penalty`→FARTCOIN's 8.0; `get_momentum_floor`→default 50; `get_trail_floor`→default 0.015; V2→not calibrated). Those four dicts are now `Partial<Record<…>>` and the UI renders the fallback with a **"(default)"** marker (or "Not calibrated (V2)") rather than fabricating a per-ticker value — keeps the "verified-against-bot-source" contract honest, no drift.
- **No standalone "per-ticker analytics card" (WR/avg-win/best-trade) and no "live board" exist** in the autotrader zone — the prompt described surfaces that aren't in the current UI (`trades/live-board.tsx` is orphaned dead code, its API routes deleted in Wave E2). The real per-ticker surface is the Config-tab `PerTickerPanel` (handled above) and the Recent-tab ticker filter chips (`recent-tab.tsx:SACRED_TICKERS` +5). The "scanning N tickers" indicator is the static `scalper-header.tsx` label `5 tickers → 10 tickers`.
- **`/api/prices` route — kPEPE casing fix.** HL's `allMids` keys the 1000×-PEPE perp as mixed-case `"kPEPE"`, but the route uppercased all requested symbols (`"KPEPE"`) → no match. Made symbol matching **case-insensitive** and the response **case-preserving** (keyed by the caller's exact string), so `prices["kPEPE"]` resolves from Hyperliquid at the correct k-denominated price. All 3 callers (`watchlist-grid`, `active-position-card`, `PriceStrip`) read prices back by the same string they pass, so the change is caller-safe. CoinGecko backups added for XRP→ripple / NEAR→near / SUI→sui; **kPEPE intentionally HL-only** (CoinGecko `pepe` is the raw, ~1000×-too-low price). XRP/DOGE/NEAR/SUI resolve directly from HL.

### 2026-05-27 — D4: Shadow page overhaul — compact cards + Active/Dormant tabs + function sections
- **`/intel?tab=shadow` rewritten from long-scroll grid → compact, tabbed, function-grouped layout.** The old `<ShadowSection>` (17 full-size `<ShadowTableCard>` × 4-up grid, ~7-10 mobile screens of scroll) replaced by `<ShadowOverview>` (~3-4 screens) per Ghost's "too big, too much scroll — make it glanceable" brief. All 25 shadows visible across the two tabs (20 ACTIVE + 5 DORMANT, matches A1 §9 inventory exactly; 0 BROKEN at deploy).
- **Active/Dormant `<TabBar>` at top.** Count badges per tab (`Active (N)` includes the Shadow Scoring hero in the N; `Dormant (M)` only shows function sections that have dormant shadows). React state, not URL params — this is a nested tab inside the Intel SHADOW sub-tab; URL `?tab=shadow` continues to route to the SHADOW sub-tab, and the inner Active/Dormant state lives in `useState`.
- **Function sub-sections** via `<CollapsibleSection>` + `rightSlot={<Pill intent="active|warn" size="sm">{count}</Pill>}` — per C1 Ghost ruling 2 (no `<CategorySection>` primitive built; `<CollapsibleSection>` is the documented single source of truth). Order: Entry → Exit → Scoring → Risk → Data. `defaultOpen` heuristic: ≤5 items → open, >5 → collapsed (current data has Entry=6 + Exit=6 active so those start collapsed; everything else open).
- **`<ShadowScoringHero>` at the top of the Active tab.** Dedicated card for the HMM+DS pipeline (the 22 K+ row core scoring system), visually distinct via `card-elevated` + `border-l-4 border-l-accent-cyan-soft-strong`. Shows Method · Rows · Ready · Last Score in a 4-up `<MetricTile>` grid + progress bar (re-uses the existing hero's metric set from `shadow-section.tsx`). To populate Method / Ready / progress, `<ShadowOverview>` fetches `/api/intel/shadow` in parallel with `/api/shadow/registry` (`Promise.all`) — registry alone doesn't carry `model_method` / `rows_required_for_analysis` / `ready_for_analysis`. **`shadow_scoring` is stripped from the Scoring function section** to avoid visual duplication (Ghost decision at D4 Phase 0 gate). Scoring section therefore shows 3 active (`shadow_scorer_v2`, `calibrator_audit`, `calibration_v2_audit`) and 1 dormant (`group_weight_shadow`).
- **`<CompactShadowCard>` (C1 primitive) — adapter, not modification.** Registry fields (`display`, `table_name`, `rows`, `rows_48h`, `latest_write` ISO, `status` `"ACTIVE"|"DORMANT"|"BROKEN"`) map to card props (`name`, `tableName`, `totalRows`, `rows48h`, `latestAge` relative, `status` `"active"|"dormant"`) in a local `adaptCard()` helper. `BROKEN` collapses to `"dormant"` for card visual + gets surfaced via the Dormant tab. `fmtAge()` mirrors the proven UTC-Z normalizer from `shadow-table-card.tsx` (SQLite naive timestamps + ISO-offset tolerated). Each card wrapped in a `border-l-2` div with activity-driven left color (>100 rows/48h → cyan-soft-strong, 10-100 → cyan-soft/50, 1-9 → fg-faint, 0/dormant → border-subtle) — color coding achieved without modifying the C1 shared primitive.
- **Sort:** Active tab sorts each function section by `rows_48h` DESC (most active first); Dormant tab sorts by `rows` DESC (most historical data first). Per Phase 3 spec.
- **Accordion expand intentionally NOT enforced** — Ghost decision at D4 Phase 0 gate. `<CompactShadowCard>`'s built-in self-managed `expanded` state stays. Lifting state would require modifying the C1 shared primitive (`expanded` + `onToggle` props), which Ghost ruled against. Multiple cards can be expanded concurrently — acceptable for a glance-heavy compact layout.
- **`<ShadowSection>` + `<ShadowTableCard>` left on disk, no longer imported.** Per Phase 4 hard constraint ("Don't delete old shadow components. Stop importing them, but leave the files. Cleanup is a separate prompt."). The only import-site, `intel-zone-view.tsx:33`, now points at `<ShadowOverview>`. `/api/intel/shadow` route + `query_shadow_status.py` stay live (the hero uses the route).
- **Files (new, 2):** `src/components/intel/shadow-overview.tsx` (~210 lines), `src/components/intel/shadow-scoring-hero.tsx` (~110 lines). **(modified, 2):** `src/components/intel/intel-zone-view.tsx` (swap `ShadowSection` → `ShadowOverview` import + 1 JSX line; docblock entry for D4), `CLAUDE.md` (this entry).
- **Untouched on purpose:** `src/components/intel/notes-section.tsx`, `LessonsSection`, `JournalSection` — D4 scope is the SHADOW sub-tab only; the other 3 Intel sub-tabs are byte-identical. C1 shared primitives (`CompactShadowCard`, `CollapsibleSection`, `TabBar`, `Pill`) consumed via barrel `@/components/ui` — none modified. No new API routes, no new Python helpers (registry endpoint shipped in B1c). No bot file touched. No service restart. No `auto_config` flag flip. No DB schema change.
- **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (clean). `npm run build` clean — `/intel` route **9.33 kB** (was 7.88 kB at 2026-05-26 baseline; +1.45 kB net for the new tab/hero/grouping vs replaced shadow-section grid — old components still on disk but no longer imported, so the +1.45 kB is the marginal cost of the new layout). Sacred 12/12 untouched (Hub-only wave).
- **D4 commit prefix:** `feat(D4-shadow)`. Hub `feat/D4-shadow` worktree at `/home/trevor/worktrees/D4`. NOT merged — awaiting D1/D2/D3 to complete + Ghost's `scripts/worktree_merge_all.sh` orchestration per the wave spec.

### 2026-05-27 — D3: AutoTrader Activity tab — paginated change_log + source filters
- **Wave D3 of the AutoTrader/Shadow overhaul.** The Activity sub-tab on `/autotrader?tab=activity` now ships the paginated `change_log` readout C1's placeholder reserved. Parallel with D1 / D2 / D4 — each in its own worktree off the C1 commit.
- **`src/components/autotrader-v2/activity-tab.tsx` rewritten** (~210 lines; was a 17-line placeholder). Fetches `GET /api/auto/activity?limit=50&offset=<n>&source_type=<v>` (B1c endpoint), newest first. State: `entries[]`, `total`, `loading`, `initialLoad`, `error`, `chipLabel`. `hasMore = entries.length < total`. Defensive force-stop if the API ever returns an empty page while still claiming more rows.
- **Single `<FilterChips>` bar** — 6 chips: **All · UI · Discord · Autonomous · CC Prompt · CLI-Script**. Display label → API `source_type` mapping (verified against `audit_logger.py:59` SOURCE_TYPES enum `{UI, Discord, Autonomous, Direct-Edit, CLI-Script, Trigger, API, unknown}`): `CC Prompt → "API"` (CC sessions write through the Hub API); other chips map 1:1; `All` sends no `source_type` param. `Trigger` rows (close_audit_log fires; 5/9 of today's rows) intentionally not chipped — surface under `All` only, per Ghost ruling at THOUGHTS gate. Switching chips resets `entries`/`total` and refetches from offset 0 (via the `[sourceValue]` effect — cancelled flag protects against late responses).
- **Infinite scroll via `IntersectionObserver`** (per prompt §1.4). A 1px sentinel `<div ref={sentinelRef}/>` sits after the last `<ActivityRow>`. On intersection (`rootMargin: "120px"` so the next page kicks in just before the sentinel is fully in view), `loadMore()` fires with `offset = entries.length`. `loadingRef` guards re-entry; the observer effect re-creates the IO on every `[hasMore, loading, initialLoad, loadMore]` change so the auto-cascade fills tall viewports without a stuck state. Cleanup `observer.disconnect()` in every teardown.
- **Empty / loading / error states.** `initialLoad` → `<Skeleton h-32 w-full/>`. `entries.length === 0 && !filterActive` → `EmptyState` "No activity recorded yet. Changes will appear here once you edit configs or toggles." `entries.length === 0 && filterActive` → `EmptyState "No <Chip> activity found" · "Try a different source filter."` Mid-pagination → `<Skeleton h-4 w-32/>` strip below the sentinel. End-of-feed → `— No more entries —` sans-uppercase divider. API/network errors → red banner above the list (`accent-red/5` bg + `accent-red/40` border).
- **Each entry rendered via C1's `<ActivityRow>` shared primitive** (unmodified, imported from `@/components/ui` barrel). Built-ins reused as-shipped: UTC-Z timestamp parser → relative `Ns/m/h/d ago` with raw stamp in `title` tooltip; expand-to-show actor + source + promptId + notes `<dl>`; source `<Pill intent>` map (UI → blue-chip · Discord → mid-cap · Autonomous → warn · CC|Claude → active · else → neutral default). `Trigger`/`CLI-Script`/`API` source-types render with the default neutral pill — per Ghost ruling at THOUGHTS gate, acceptable for D3; extending the intent map is a future surgical edit to ActivityRow, not D3 scope. Null-`key` rows fall back to `<table_name>#<row_id>` for display; null `new_value` shows `—`.
- **State preservation across tab switches** — `<AutoZoneView subtab="activity">` mounts/unmounts on tab changes, so component remount refetches from offset 0 and resets the filter chip to "All". Acceptable per prompt §2.1 ("preserves state OR refetches — either acceptable").
- **Verified.** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0. `npx next build` clean — `/autotrader` route **13.2 kB** (was 12.3 kB at C1; +0.9 kB for the new tab). Live `query_activity.py 5 0` against production `trevor.db` returned 5 of 9 rows (4 `Autonomous` from `DISCOVERED_TICKERS` updates + 5 `Trigger` from close_audit_log fires) — UI handles both source-types and the null-`old_value` shape gracefully. **Service restart deferred** — never restarted from inside a worktree workflow per established Hub-wave pattern; D1 / D2 / D4 will report and the bundle picks up at the next coordinated restart.
- **Untouched on purpose:** B1c `/api/auto/activity` route + `query_activity.py` helper (consumed as-shipped — they were built for D3). `<ActivityRow>` + `<FilterChips>` shared primitives (C1 infra; D3 imports from `@/components/ui` — zero duplication). `<AutoZoneView>` dispatcher + the other 4 tab files. Bot files, sacred files, `trevor.db` schema, design tokens. No new npm deps. No flag flips. No DB schema changes.
- **Path note** (carry-over from C1's D-wave note): the prompt brief referred to `src/components/auto/ActivityTab.tsx` and a `shared` dir — actual paths are `src/components/autotrader-v2/activity-tab.tsx` (kebab-case) and `src/components/ui/` (barrel `index.ts`). Already correct at the worktree from C1's commit.
- **Hub `master` commit prefix:** `feat(D3-activity)` — committed in `/home/trevor/worktrees/D3` on branch `feat/D3-activity`, **not pushed, not merged this prompt** (D1 / D2 / D4 still in flight; merge wave follows).

### 2026-05-27 — D2: Control Center sub-tab — 85 toggles grouped by category + Tier 1/2/3 confirm tiers (worktree)

- **D2 wave of the AutoTrader/Shadow overhaul.** Replaces the C1 `<ControlTab>` placeholder with a live-editable interface for every `auto_config` boolean. Pause/Resume at the top, 7 collapsible category sections in the middle, recent toggle changes from B1b `change_log` at the bottom.
- **Filter discipline.** The 4 dedicated-endpoint keys (`AUTO_TRADER_ENABLED`, `EMERGENCY_KILLSWITCH`, `LIVE_PARTIALS_ENABLED`, `CONFIRM_CYCLES_PROMOTED`) are filtered OUT of the toggle list — each already has its own dedicated card / pill / page surface. The control-full PATCH refuses them with 400; rendering them here would be misleading duplication. Footer advisory documents which keys are hidden and where they live. 89 − 4 = **85 visible toggles**.
- **Layout (top → bottom):**
  1. `<AutoTraderToggleCard />` — reused as-is from the Dashboard tab (Rule 32 carve-out, owns its own 2-tap BottomSheet + audit + 30s poll). Per Ghost decision: don't extract a separate PauseResumeSection — the card is already perfect.
  2. LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED state banner — UNLOCKED (mint, write-mode advisory) vs LOCKED (gold, with copy-paste SQL to enable). Banner is non-interactive; the gate flip path is a deliberate Ghost decision via SQL or CLI.
  3. Toast strip — `{ok|warn|err}` 4s lifetime, surfaces the PATCH result.
  4. 7 `<CollapsibleSection>`s in A1 §3 order: **Execution (10) · Signal Gates (28) · Critic Stack (8) · Calibration (5) · Risk (16) · Experimental (6) · Misc (16)** — actual visible counts after dedicated-key filter: **8 · 26 · 8 · 5 · 16 · 6 · 16 = 85** (4 keys filtered out: 2 from Execution — `AUTO_TRADER_ENABLED`, `EMERGENCY_KILLSWITCH`; 2 from Signal Gates — `LIVE_PARTIALS_ENABLED`, `CONFIRM_CYCLES_PROMOTED`). Default-open: Execution + Signal Gates (highest impact, scanned first); the rest collapsed. `rightSlot` shows a red `<Pill intent="error">{N} T1</Pill>` when the category has Tier-1 keys, then a blue-chip count.
  5. Hidden-keys advisory card.
  6. `<RecentTogglesSection />` — bottom card reading `/api/auto/activity?limit=50&table_name=auto_config`, client-filters to `new_value ∈ {true, false}` (drops non-bool flips like `DISCOVERED_TICKERS` JSON), renders top 10 via `<ActivityRow>`. "View all activity →" link routes to `/autotrader?tab=activity` (D3 placeholder for now). 30s poll.
- **Sensitivity tier behavior (C1 `<ToggleSwitch>` carries the burden):**
  - **Tier 1 (destructive, A1 §9):** tap → `<ConfirmModal requireReason destructive>` opens. Confirm disabled until non-empty reason. Reason flows into the PATCH body and lands in `change_log.notes` via the new helper extension.
  - **Tier 2 (sensitive):** tap → in-row 2s gold warning string `Toggling "X" — sensitive flag.` then PATCH fires.
  - **Tier 3 (free):** tap → PATCH fires immediately.
- **B1c PATCH extension (additive, 3 lines TS + 2 lines Python):** `PATCH /api/auto/control-full/[key]` now accepts optional `reason` in body, passed as 5th argv to `write_control_value.py`. The helper threads it to `audit_config_write(notes=reason or "D2 toggle flip")`. Backward compatible — the 4-argv signature still works for any caller that doesn't pass reason. Per Ghost ruling 1 at the THOUGHTS gate: extend B1c, don't rewrite or stash reason in author.
- **423 handling:** when `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED=false`, PATCH returns 423 (not 403 as the prompt brief said — B1c uses 423 Locked). UI catches it in `handleToggle`, surfaces a gold warn toast `"Editing locked — set LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED=true in auto_config to enable toggle writes."` and throws so the ToggleSwitch optimistic state rolls back. Toggle switches themselves render `disabled` when `liveEdit === false` for visual confirmation that taps are non-productive.
- **No `<CategorySection>` primitive created** — per C1 ruling, `<CollapsibleSection title=... rightSlot={<Pill>{count}</Pill>}>` is the chosen pattern; D2 follows it.
- **Files (new, 1):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/recent-toggles-section.tsx`. **(modified, 3):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/control-tab.tsx` (placeholder → real D2 component, ~310 LOC) · `src/app/api/auto/control-full/[key]/route.ts` (+5 lines: read optional `reason` from body, conditional argv append) · `write_control_value.py` (+3 lines: read argv[4], thread to audit `notes`; docstring updated). No bot files, no DB writes, no sacred file touches.
- **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (zero new errors). `python3 write_control_value.py SHADOW_LOG_NEUTRAL false ghost "test reason"` with `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED=false` returns exit 3 → JSON `{ok:false, gate_locked:true, status:423}` (gate path still fires correctly with the new arg). Live API verified: `/api/auto/control-full` returns 89 toggles across 7 categories with tier distribution 7/25/57. `/api/auto/activity?table_name=auto_config` returns the 16 current `change_log` rows (post-filter to bool yields the toggle flips).
- **Activation path:** merge `feat/D2-control` into master (Ghost runs `bash scripts/worktree_merge_all.sh --restart trevor-dashboard.service` after all D1-D4 wrap). Then `UPDATE auto_config SET value='true' WHERE key='LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED';` (no service restart needed; the helper reads it per-request). To disable: same SQL with `value='false'`.
- **Hub `master` commit prefix:** `feat(D2-control)`. Bot side untouched. Worktree: `/home/trevor/worktrees/D2` on branch `feat/D2-control`. **Sacred 9/9 untouched.** No flag flips. No new npm deps. No DB schema changes.

### 2026-05-27 — D1: Config sub-tab — editable thresholds + per-ticker panel
- **Config tab is real.** Replaces the C1 placeholder at `/autotrader?tab=config` with a fully-wired editor for the 60 non-boolean `auto_config` rows surfaced by `/api/auto/config-full`. Fetch + 60 s auto-refresh, debounced search (200 ms), grouped by A1 §2 category (Capital → Signal → Exit → Risk → Per-Ticker → Calibration → Execution → Misc), one `<EditableField>` per row with type-correct validation (int / float / text + JSON-parse for `type=json`). Calibration section auto-hides when empty (currently 0 rows). Honest about its own write-gate: when `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED=false` a top gold banner reads "Live edit disabled — flip LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED first"; PATCH attempts still return HTTP 423 from the server, and the shared `handleSave` closure surfaces that as the inline EditableField error.
- **PerTickerPanel is the Per-Ticker category's renderer.** Replaces the flat key-value list with a ticker-centric view (`<FilterChips>` for BTC / ETH / SOL / HYPE / FARTCOIN). For the selected ticker it shows: confidence thresholds (quiet/normal/active from `TICKER_THRESHOLDS`); shadow thresholds V2 (`RECALIBRATED_THRESHOLDS_V2`); direction penalty; momentum floor; trail floor; max leverage (🔒 immutable per Ghost ruling 4). Those six values are **source-only** — they live in Python source (`ticker_thresholds.py` / `auto_trader/config.py` / `auto_trader/monitor.py` / `auto_trader/exit_helpers.py`), not `auto_config`, so the Hub mirrors them as read-only with the `Source-code config — edit via CC prompt` tooltip. Below them, an "Auto-Config Overrides" group surfaces the three editable DB-side per-ticker rows: **ALO Disabled** (`HapticButton` that toggles the ticker in/out of `ALO_DISABLED_TICKERS` JSON array), **Hard Stop PCT** (`<EditableField type="float">` that round-trips through `TICKER_HARD_STOP_PCT_JSON` dict), **Leverage V2 Seed** (same pattern for `LEVERAGE_V2_INTERMEDIATE_JSON`). Bottom of the panel lists `DISCOVERED_TICKERS` (67-row autonomous list) as read-only with a warn pill.
- **Shared primitives only** — D1 imports `<EditableField>`, `<CollapsibleSection>` (the `<CategorySection>` replacement per the C1 Ghost ruling — `rightSlot={<Pill size="sm">{count}</Pill>}` for category badges), `<FilterChips>`, `<Pill>`, `<Card>`/`<CardHeader>`/`<CardTitle>`, `<Skeleton>`, `<EmptyState>`, `<HapticButton>` — every primitive came from the C1 shared set. **No new ui/ primitives**, no design-token additions, no new npm deps.
- **Single save closure.** Both the per-category EditableFields and the PerTickerPanel funnel through the same `handleSave(key, newValue)` callback in `ConfigTab`. Maps PATCH responses: 200 → refetch (so `updated_at` stays fresh); 400 → `throw new Error(payload.error)` → EditableField inline error; 423 → `throw new Error("Editing disabled — flip LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED first.")`; other non-2xx → throw `payload.error ?? HTTP {status}`. JSON dict round-trip: `saveDictEntry()` in PerTickerPanel mutates the selected ticker's entry (or `delete`s it on empty input) and writes back the full serialized dict via the same closure.
- **Path discrepancies in the D1 brief — corrected per C1 carry-forward:** brief said `src/components/auto/ConfigTab.tsx` (PascalCase) → real path `src/components/autotrader-v2/config-tab.tsx` (kebab-case, matches every other tab + the auto-zone-view dispatcher). Brief said `CategorySection` exists as a shared primitive → it does NOT (C1 Ghost ruling — reuse `<CollapsibleSection rightSlot={<Pill>…</Pill>}>` instead). Brief said gate-locked is HTTP 403 → actual is HTTP 423 (matches the B1c spec + write_config_value.py exit-3 mapping).
- **Files (new, 1):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/per-ticker-panel.tsx` (~270 lines; source-of-truth dicts hardcoded from the verified bot-side files, JSON round-trip handlers, ALO toggle with inline pending/error). **(modified, 1):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/config-tab.tsx` — full overwrite of the C1 placeholder (~270 lines: fetch + poll + debounced search + grouped CollapsibleSection render + gate banner + skeleton + empty state). **`auto-zone-view.tsx` untouched** — already routes `case "config"` → `<ConfigTab />`, no wiring change needed.
- **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (clean). `next build` clean — `/autotrader` route **15.8 kB** (was 12.3 kB at C1, +3.5 kB for the two new components). Zero warnings / errors during build. Component-map row `autotrader-v2/` count 14 → 15 files (per-ticker-panel.tsx added).
- **Verification gaps (honest):** **No service restart** (per worktree-isolated wave pattern — restart deferred to `worktree_merge_all.sh` post-merge). **No live API smoke** against the running Hub (the live server still serves the C1 placeholder until merge + restart). **No 423/400/200 path exercise against the production endpoint** — that happens post-merge with `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED` flipped briefly by Ghost. Static + build verification only inside the worktree.

If the bot-side files are edited via a CC prompt without paired D1 updates, the Hub display will drift. Acceptable trade-off vs. building a Python-source-parser endpoint: these dicts change rarely + observability is worth the staleness risk + alternatives (server-side import or AST parse) cross the Hub→bot read boundary and add fragility.

Other autotrader-v2 components (Dashboard, Recent stay byte-identical; Control/Activity remain placeholders for D2/D3; Per-Ticker rendering on the existing `<ConfigCard>` dashboard tile is unchanged). All ui/ primitives. No new design tokens. No flag flips. No DB schema changes. No service restart. No `auto_config` writes during testing (validation done via tsc + build only — no test rows to clean up).
